Method: DeepInvert是一种半监督嵌入反演攻击，核心洞察是未标记的混淆嵌入即使经过扰动仍保留可利用的语义结构。该方法结合了（1）在标记影子数据上的监督训练，与（2）在未标记目标嵌入上的新型无监督一致性目标，通过混合训练流程交替进行。此外还设计了防御感知的自适应机制，可扩展到基于编码器和自回归架构的多种混淆机制。

Result: 在九种防御、五个任务和四种模型架构上的实验表明，DeepInvert在大多数防御上优于现有攻击。针对ObfusLM，DeepInvert实现了73.5%的top-1 token恢复率，而此前最佳方法仅为26.2%。结果揭示了任务依赖的权衡：能保留足够效用信号的混淆方案也保留了足够的可反演结构；而抗反演的方案则导致效用崩溃。在简单分类任务上，部分基于差分隐私的防御可同时保持两者。

Conclusion: 作者呼吁重新评估基于混淆的防御这一类方案的安全性，指出该类防御提供的隐私保护在实际中远弱于预期，需要重新审视其作为轻量级隐私保护手段的有效性。

Abstract: Cloud-based language model services routinely process prompts containing sensitive information. Obfuscation-based defenses---including ObfusLM, SentinelLMs, TextObfuscator, and DPNR---mitigate this risk by transforming prompt representations before transmission, offering a lightweight alternative to cryptographic solutions. We show these defenses provide far less protection than previously believed.
  We present DeepInvert, a semi-supervised embedding inversion attack that recovers original tokens from obfuscated representations with higher accuracy than prior methods. The key insight is that unlabeled obfuscated embeddings retain exploitable semantic structure despite perturbation. DeepInvert combines supervised training on labeled shadow data with a novel unsupervised consistency objective over unlabeled target embeddings, alternating between the two via a mixed training pipeline. Defense-aware adaptations further extend the attack to diverse obfuscation mechanisms across encoder-based and autoregressive architectures.
  Experiments on nine defenses, five tasks, and four model architectures show that DeepInvert outperforms prior attacks on most defenses. Against ObfusLM, DeepInvert achieves 73.5\% top-1 token recovery versus 26.2\% for the previous best. Our results reveal a task-dependent tension: obfuscation schemes preserving enough signal for utility also retain sufficient structure for inversion, while schemes resisting inversion collapse utility. On simpler classification tasks, some DP-based defenses can maintain both. We call for a re-evaluation of this defense class.

</details>


### [208] [Checked-In Secret Detection: Strings Are All You Need](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04523)
*Zhengdong Huang,Kevin Li,Jinqiu Yang,Yepang Liu,Lili Wei*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出StringGroup上下文提取算法和基于Transformer的Secretron工具，通过聚焦字符串字面量来检测源代码中的硬编码秘密，在SecretBench数据集上达到98.74%的F1分数，并在真实环境中检测出48个此前未知的密钥。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 源代码中的硬编码秘密是严重的安全漏洞，现有基于正则表达式的方法因秘密缺乏可识别模式而精度和召回率不佳；基于上下文的检测方法面临混淆鲁棒性差、跨语言泛化困难及上下文冗长嘈杂三大挑战。

Method: 提出StringGroup算法，核心洞察是字符串是代码语义的关键信息源，具有更高的上下文密度、混淆鲁棒性和语言无关性。通过将分析范围缩小到字符串字面量，仅用33.2%的原始上下文就保留80%以上的语义信息。在此基础上设计Secretron工具，结合StringGroup方法和Transformer模型进行上下文感知的秘密检测。

Result: 在SecretBench数据集上达到98.74%的F1分数，在混淆和跨语言场景下表现出强鲁棒性，超越基于LLM的最先进基线方法。在真实环境中成功从26个应用中检测出48个此前未知的秘密密钥。

Conclusion: 字符串字面量可以作为秘密检测的高质量上下文来源，StringGroup方法通过简单有效的模式修改显著提升了检测性能，Secretron在实际部署中验证了其实用价值。

Abstract: Hardcoded secrets in source code pose critical security vulnerabilities which can be easily exploited by malicious adversaries. Existing regex-based detection approaches suffer from fundamental limitations, as secrets often lack identifiable patterns, resulting in poor precision and recall. Recent studies have explored context-aware detection methods, as surrounding code can reveal the purpose of candidate strings. However, these methods confront three key challenges: (1) obfuscation robustness where models over-rely on easily obfuscated identifiers, (2) cross-language generalization difficulties due to uneven training data distribution, and (3) lengthy and noisy context that introduces excessive irrelevant tokens and slows inference. We observe that strings serve as a critical information source for code semantics, offering superior contextual density, obfuscation robustness, and language independence. Based on this insight, we propose StringGroup, a novel context extraction algorithm that mines strings surrounding potential secrets. By introducing a relatively simple modification to existing patterns that narrows the analysis specifically to string literals, the method achieves significant gains. With only 33.2% of the original context, it preserves over 80% of semantic information and significantly improves the signal-to-noise ratio for secret detection. We further design a context-aware secret detection tool, Secretron, based on StringGroup methods and Transformer model. Evaluation on the SecretBench dataset demonstrates high accuracy with 98.74% F1-score and strong robustness under obfuscation and cross-language scenarios, outperforming state-of-the-art LLM-based baselines. We deploy our tool in real-world environments and successfully detect 48 previously unknown secret keys from 26 applications, demonstrating the practical effectiveness of our approach.

</details>


### [209] [Breadcrumbing Search Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04565)
*Xuebin Li,Hanqing Zhao,Siyuan Liang,Kejiang Chen,Weiming Zhang,Dacheng Tao,Nenghai Yu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文发现LLM搜索代理的安全边界不仅在于单页注入，还在于搜索/页面观察通道可被中介接口反复操纵。提出Authority-Chain Hijack (ACH)策略，通过跨轨迹协调的受控搜索结果构建虚假证据链，达到55.9% ASR；并提出Trace-Guided Strategy Evolution (TGSE)自动优化攻击策略，最高达71.4%/95.0% ASR。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有搜索代理安全研究主要关注静态网页内容注入，但现代代理会发起后续查询并交叉验证多个来源，单一注入页面往往被稀释或拒绝。因此需要研究更隐蔽、可跨轨迹协调的搜索中介通道攻击威胁。

Method: 在受限的"工具中介"威胁模型下，每次查询仅附加一个受控结果即可显著提升攻击成功率，前提是跨代理执行轨迹协调证据。提出ACH（Authority-Chain Hijack）策略，将孤立的搜索结果与页面内容操纵串联为连贯的虚假证据链；并提出TGSE（Trace-Guided Strategy Evolution），从执行轨迹中自动改进攻击策略。

Result: ACH在SafeSearch完整测试集上达到55.9% ASR / 83.3% MaxN ASR，为所有基线中最高。TGSE最强单一设置在留出评估中达到71.4% / 95.0% ASR/MaxN ASR。

Conclusion: 搜索与页面观察的传递通道是脆弱的安边界，跨轨迹协调的操纵可显著增强攻击效果；基于轨迹驱动的策略进化可取代人工设计，自动生成更强大的攻击策略。

Abstract: LLM-based search agents are widely used for information-seeking tasks, but their reliance on external tool returns introduces a critical security risk: web content retrieved during execution is untrusted, exposing agents to prompt injection and goal hijacking. Prior work on search-agent safety primarily focuses on static web-content injection, but modern agents issue follow-up queries and cross-check competing sources, so a single injected page is often diluted or rejected. We show that the channel delivering search and page observations is a fragile security boundary: beyond exposing the agent to a single poisoned page, a mediated search interface can repeatedly steer how the agent gathers evidence and forms its final answer. Under a constrained tool-intermediary threat model, appending only one controlled result per query can substantially increase attack success when the evidence is coordinated across the agent's trajectory. We study this setting with a strategy-driven long-horizon attack system and introduce Authority-Chain Hijack (ACH), an expert-refined strategy that turns isolated search-result and page-content manipulations into a coherent evidence chain across seemingly corroborating sources. ACH achieves the highest Overall ASR among all baselines, reaching 55.9% / 83.3% ASR / MaxN ASR on the full SafeSearch test split. We further introduce Trace-Guided Strategy Evolution (TGSE), which automatically improves attacker strategies from execution traces, replacing manual redesign with trace-driven refinement; its strongest single setting reaches 71.4% / 95.0% in held-out evaluation.

</details>


### [210] [Adaptive Intrusion Detection System using Transformer-Based Neural Networks and Continual Learning Approach with Adversarial Investigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04602)
*Azizi Ariffin,Afif Haris,Faiz Zaki,Hazim Hanif,Nor Badrul Anuar*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于表格Transformer编码器与类平衡经验回放缓冲区的自适应入侵检测系统（IDS）框架，在类增量与类实例增量（CII）场景下分别达到0.9994和0.9989的准确率，显著优于现有基线；同时揭示了回放缓冲区作为攻击面所带来的安全隐患——标签翻转可导致模型完全失效，后门攻击可在保持高整体准确率的同时实现95%的触发器攻击成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有IDS在部署后因威胁分布漂移而性能退化，微调会导致灾难性遗忘，从头重训练计算代价过高。现有的基于回放的经验持续学习方法存在两个问题：一是将良性流量不现实地限制在单个早期任务中，二是忽视了回放缓冲区本身可能成为攻击面。

Method: 提出了一个将表格Transformer编码器与类平衡经验回放缓冲区耦合的自适应IDS框架，每次更新时回放良性流量以稳定决策边界。引入了类实例增量（CII）场景（良性流与新攻击同时出现）作为更真实的压力测试，并对缓冲区进行标签翻转和隐蔽后门投毒攻击测试。

Result: 在CICIDS2017基准上，框架在传统类增量设置下达到0.9994准确率，在CII场景下达到0.9989，遗忘可忽略不计，大幅超越顺序微调（0.0052）、EWC（0.0324）、LwF（0.0699）和iCaRL（0.8770）基线。然而标签翻转攻击在1%预算下使模型准确率崩溃至0.0053，后门攻击在维持0.97整体准确率的同时使触发器流量攻击成功率达95%。

Conclusion: 在每次经验更新中注入良性流量对防止遗忘至关重要，回放缓冲区也使恢复接近联合训练性能成为可能（适度的回放预算即可实现），但确保缓冲区完整性是严格的操作性要求，缓冲区安全性必须作为系统设计的一等公民对待。

Abstract: Network intrusion detection systems (IDS) trained on fixed traffic snapshots decay silently after deployment as threat distributions shift. Fine-tuning models on new attacks triggers catastrophic forgetting, while retraining from scratch is computationally infeasible. Replay-based continual learning counters this, but existing methods unrealistically confine benign traffic to a single early task and ignore the replay buffer as a potential attack surface. To address this, we present an adaptive IDS framework coupling a tabular transformer encoder with a class balanced experience replay buffer that replays benign traffic at every update to stabilize decision boundaries. We introduce the class-instance incremental (CII) scenario where benign flows reappear alongside new attacks as a more faithful stress test, and probe the buffer with overt label flipping and stealthy backdoor poisoning attacks. On the CICIDS2017 benchmark, our framework achieved 0.9994 accuracy under the traditional class incremental setup and 0.9989 under CII, with negligible forgetting, drastically outperforming sequential fine-tuning (0.0052), EWC (0.0324), LwF (0.0699), and iCaRL (0.8770) baselines. While injecting benign traffic into every experience proves essential for preventing forgetting, the replay buffer introduces critical vulnerabilities. Label-flipping collapses the model entirely (0.0053 accuracy at a 1% budget), and the backdoor maintains 0.97 overall accuracy while driving the attack success rate on trigger flows to 95%, evading standard monitoring. Ultimately, while a modest replay budget recovers near-joint-training performance, ensuring buffer integrity emerges as a strict operational requirement.

</details>


### [211] [Hidden Ciphers and Where to Find Them: Static Discovery and Assessment of Cryptographic Assets in Software](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04857)
*Christian Näther,Eduard Hirsch*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于静态分析的密码资产发现与评估方法，通过引入Crypto-Material、Crypto-Artifacts和Crypto-Invocations三类分类体系，构建可扩展的扫描器无关规则库，并实现可生成CBOM（密码资产清单）输出的静态扫描器。在合成基准和真实环境（10个部署服务、57,610个文件）中分别达到0.75的F1分数和6分钟内完成扫描的效果，发现了370个密码资产、6个CVE关联漏洞和52个后量子迁移候选。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代软件系统依赖密码学保障数据安全，但组织往往缺乏对源代码、配置、依赖和密码文件中部署密码学的结构化视图，这种可见性的缺失增加了安全治理和后量子迁移规划的复杂性。

Method: 提出Crypto-Material、Crypto-Artifacts和Crypto-Invocations的三级分类体系，并据此派生出可扩展的、与扫描器无关的规则库；实现一个应用这些规则的静态扫描器，生成面向CBOM的输出结果。

Result: 在合成基准上资产发现的F1分数为0.75，正确标注91%的预期弱点和漏洞；在真实环境中6分钟内处理57,610个文件，发现370个密码资产、6个CVE关联漏洞和52个后量子迁移候选。

Conclusion: 分类驱动的静态发现方法能够为安全治理和后量子迁移规划提供实用的密码学透明度，具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: Modern software systems rely on cryptography for data protection, authentication, and trust establishment, yet organizations often lack a structured view of the cryptography deployed across source code, configuration, dependencies, and cryptographic files. This lack of visibility complicates security governance and post-quantum migration planning. This paper presents a static approach for discovering and assessing cryptographic assets in software systems. We introduce a classification of Crypto-Material, Crypto-Artifacts, and Crypto-Invocations, derive an extensible scanner-independent rule repository from it, and implement a static scanner that applies these rules to produce CBOM-oriented output. We evaluate the approach on a synthetic benchmark with known ground truth and on a real-world infrastructure of ten deployed services. The scanner achieves an F1 score of 0.75 for asset discovery and correctly annotates 91% of expected weaknesses and vulnerabilities. In the realworld setting, it processes 57 610 files in under six minutes and discovers 370 cryptographic assets, including six CVE-linked vulnerabilities and 52 post-quantum migration candidates. Real-world coverage is assessed against a manually compiled reference list rather than an exhaustive one. These results show that classification-driven static discovery can provide practical cryptographic transparency for governance and post-quantum migration planning.

</details>


### [212] [Blockchain Empowered Trustworthy Agent Networks: Foundations, Taxonomy, and Future Directions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04626)
*Liehuang Zhu,Yuhang Li,Tianxing Wang,Zhihao Chen,Ke Li,Hongyi Liu,Yajie Wang,Lei Xu,Peng Jiang,Zijian Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文综述了从经典多智能体系统到开放智能体网络的演变（1980-2026），重点关注基于LLM的自主智能体、智能体互操作协议、智能体互联网基础设施和区块链信任机制，提出五维信任分类法，并阐明区块链作为共享信任层而非替代品的角色。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI智能体从孤立任务执行者演变为网络化自主实体，异构智能体在不同利益相关者之间交互时缺乏统一的身份、授权、审计、声誉和结算基础设施，产生了网络层面的信任危机，需要系统性的综述和分类框架来指导研究。

Method: 对1980-2026年期间的相关文献进行系统性综述，首先梳理从经典多智能体系统到开放智能体网络的演化脉络，然后提出五维信任分类法（实体与能力信任、授权与委托信任、信息与溯源信任、协调与群体鲁棒性信任、问责与结算信任），并综合映射智能体网络风险、信任需求与区块链机制之间的关系。

Result: 识别出单智能体安全机制或封闭式多智能体协调技术无法完全解决的网络级信任危机；建立了五维信任分类法；论证了区块链可提供共享身份、可验证授权、防篡改溯源、可审计协作、激励对齐和价值结算等机制；明确了区块链作为共享信任层的定位。

Conclusion: 区块链在可信智能体网络中扮演共享信任层的角色，而非替代智能体安全、语义验证、隐私保护或鲁棒推理；研究应关注智能体网络风险、信任需求与区块链机制之间的映射关系，以构建真正可信的开放智能体网络。

Abstract: AI agents are evolving from isolated task executors into networked autonomous entities that can communicate, delegate tasks, invoke tools, access external knowledge, and participate in cross-platform service and economic workflows. This evolution gives rise to open agent networks, where heterogeneous agents owned by different stakeholders interact without naturally shared infrastructures for identity, authorization, auditability, reputation, or settlement. This survey and tutorial article reviews the literature over the period 1980--2026 on the evolution from classical multi-agent systems to open agent networks, with a particular focus on LLM-based autonomous agents, agent interoperability protocols, Internet-of-Agents infrastructures, and blockchain-enabled trust mechanisms. We first review this evolution and show how the trust boundary expands from individual execution to cross-agent, cross-platform, and cross-organizational interaction. We then identify a network-level trust crisis that cannot be fully addressed by single-agent safety mechanisms or closed multi-agent coordination techniques, and develop a five-dimensional taxonomy covering entity and capability trust, authorization and delegation trust, information and provenance trust, coordination and group-robustness trust, and accountability and settlement trust. Based on this taxonomy, we examine how blockchain can provide shared identity, verifiable authorization, tamper-evident provenance, auditable collaboration, incentive alignment, and value settlement for trustworthy agent networks. We further synthesize the mapping between agent-network risks, trust requirements, and blockchain-enabled mechanisms, and clarify the role of blockchain as a shared trust layer rather than a replacement for agent security, semantic verification, privacy protection, or robust reasoning.

</details>


### [213] [MOAT: Model-Agnostic Randomized Transformations for preventing Efficiency Degradation Attacks on ViTs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04680)
*Anadi Goyal,Nandish Chattopadhyay,Chandan Karfa,Anupam Chattopadhyay,Norrathep Rattanavipanon*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: MOAT是一个模型无关的预处理防御管道，通过输入变换组合来保护高效ViT实现免受针对token剪枝的对抗性效率攻击，将GFLOPs退化限制在3.4%以内。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Vision Transformers在资源受限环境中依赖token剪枝来降低计算成本，但攻击者已开发出针对token剪枝技术的定向攻击，破坏了使ViT高效化的努力。

Method: 提出MOAT，一个模型无关的预处理防御管道，应用一系列输入变换组合，直接在输入层面运作，无需修改模型架构或token剪枝机制。

Result: 在所有评估的ViT模型上，MOAT将对抗攻击下的GFLOPs退化限制在原始未攻击模型的3.4%以内。

Conclusion: MOAT作为一种即插即用的输入预处理防御方案，能有效保护高效ViT实现免受对抗性效率攻击，同时保持模型架构不变。

Abstract: To adopt the Vision Transformers (ViTs) in resource-constrained environment, token pruning is widely used to reduce computational cost without impacting accuracy. However, adversaries have developed targeted attacks against said token pruning techniques to undermine such attempts to make ViTs efficient. In this paper, we propose MOAT, a model-agnostic pre-processing defense pipeline that applies a combination of input transformations to protect efficient ViT implementations against adversarial efficiency attacks. MOAT operates directly on the input without requiring modifications to the model architecture or token pruning mechanism. Experimental results demonstrate that, across all evaluated ViT models, MOAT limits GFLOPs degradation under adversarial attacks to within 3.4% of the original unattacked model.

</details>


### [214] [LoginTrap: Uncovering Task-Agnostic Phishing-Style Indirect Prompt Injection Attacks against LLM-based Web Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04741)
*Longtao Guo,Zelin Zhang,Kaifeng Huang,Yang Shi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出LoginTrap攻击，一种针对LLM网络代理的任务无关登录诱导攻击。攻击者通过控制网页上下文生成页面特定的间接提示注入，诱导代理在登录页面泄露用户凭据，平均端到端攻击成功率达86%，揭示了登录诱导是系统性的认证边界风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM网络代理在真实网页服务上运行时会面临登录认证这一敏感边界，恶意网页内容可以操纵代理行为，但已有工作未充分研究此类内容是否能诱导登录并导致端到端的私有数据泄露。

Method: 提出LoginTrap攻击框架，假设黑盒攻击者控制网页上下文和诱导登录流程，无需知道用户任务或代理内部机制。通过模糊测试启发式过程生成页面特定的间接注入，使登录看起来是继续执行任务的合理前置条件，从而引导代理访问受控的登录页面。

Result: 在真实网络代理执行场景下进行综合分析，LoginTrap在多种LLM后端上平均端到端攻击成功率达86%，且在多种代理架构和防御措施下依然有效。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了登录诱导是系统性的认证边界风险，为网络代理的认证感知防御研究提供了动机和方向。

Abstract: LLM-based web agents automate user tasks by observing webpages and executing browser actions on behalf of users. As these agents operate on real web services, login becomes a sensitive authentication boundary because it involves credentials and sensitive information. Existing work shows that malicious webpage content can manipulate web agent actions, but it has not fully examined whether such content can induce login and cause end-to-end private data leakage. We study this attack surface and present LoginTrap, a task-agnostic login-inducing attack against LLM-based web agents. LoginTrap assumes a black box attacker that controls the webpage context and the induced login flow without knowing the user task or web agent internals. Under this threat model, LoginTrap uses webpage context to generate page-specific indirect injections through a fuzzing-inspired process, making login appear as a plausible prerequisite for continuing the task and guiding the agent to a controlled login page. We conduct a comprehensive analysis of LoginTrap across realistic web agent executions. The results show that LoginTrap reaches 86\% average end-to-end attack success across LLM backbones and remains effective across agent architectures and defenses. These findings identify login inducement as a systematic authentication boundary risk and motivate further research on authentication-aware defenses for web agents.

</details>


### [215] [Web Cache Overflow: Exploiting Imprecise Keys for Cache Degradation and Beyond](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04744)
*Matteo Golinelli,Kaan Onarlioglu,Bruno Crispo*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究了Web缓存键定义不精确导致的安全漏洞——攻击者可通过构造多余请求生成冗余缓存条目，降低缓存效率并增加源站负载，最终可能引发拒绝服务攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Web缓存的缓存键往往由网站运营者不精确地定义，导致同一对象可映射到不同缓存键，攻击者可利用此缺陷生成大量冗余缓存条目，损害缓存有效性并增加源站负担。

Method: 在五个独立缓存代理上重现缓存退化现象，并系统评估不同参数对攻击成本和缓存命中率的影响，从而刻画攻击者成本与缓存效率之间的权衡关系。

Result: 实验表明，在易受攻击的部署中，持续生成冗余缓存副本会降低缓存效率、增加源站负载，并可能促成依赖缓存逐出的攻击，最终构成拒绝服务攻击的潜在威胁。

Conclusion: 精确的缓存键设计是最直接的缓解措施，应被视为安全最佳实践。

Abstract: Web caches support the scalability needs of contemporary web applications by storing frequently accessed objects closer to clients. Web caches are conceptually associative arrays, tracking stored objects using cache keys consisting of HTTP request fields. However, these cache keys are often imprecisely defined by website operators. This allows clients to craft a multitude of requests that target the same object, but map to different cache keys.
  In this work, we show that request elements included unnecessarily in cache keys can be abused to create redundant cache entries. In susceptible deployments, sustained generation of such redundant copies reduces cache effectiveness and increases origin load, facilitating eviction-dependent attacks. Our experiments reproduce cache degradation across five stand-alone caching proxies and characterize how these parameters affect attacker cost and cache hit rate, potentially resulting in denial-of-service attacks. We conclude that precise cache-key design is the most direct mitigation against this abuse vector and should be recognized as a security best practice.

</details>


### [216] ["Allow" to Achieve, Over-Privileged Inadvertently: The Unintended Cost of Task-Completion-Driven Pop-up Decisions in Mobile GUI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04755)
*Dongsheng Chen,Yuxuan Li,Guanhua Chen,Jiaxin Zhang,Xiangyu Zhao,Lei Ma,Xin Yao,Xuetao Wei*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了移动GUI智能体在任务执行中授予权限的能力（称为"权限素养"），通过四层权限框架和注入Android风格权限弹窗，评估了四个前沿多模态大语言模型，发现模型存在"应用信任偏差"和"任务优先覆盖"等系统性偏见，提示了将任务执行与权限授权分离的设计方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动GUI智能体在执行任务时经常遇到系统权限弹窗，但它们在仅授予任务必要权限方面的能力（Permission Literacy）尚未被系统研究。这是一个涉及安全和隐私的关键能力缺口。

Method: 构建基于任务相关性和隐私风险的四级权限框架，由三位GUI智能体安全领域专家验证场景；将Android风格权限弹窗注入真实GUI任务中，使用同步标注截图和UI树层级评估四个前沿多模态大语言模型；并进行受控干预实验，分别改变任务上下文和智能体可见的请求方身份。

Result: 同一Calendar任务中，仅将请求方从Calendar改为PiMusic，权限授予从26/32降至0/32，展现出强烈的"应用信任偏差"；固定弹窗而改变任务上下文也会显著改变授权决策，显示系统性的"任务优先覆盖"；提示干预可减少不必要授权，但效果在模型间不一致，且可能抑制合理授权。

Conclusion: 将任务执行与权限授权分离是未来工作的有前景设计方向，以缓解模型在权限授予中的系统偏差。

Abstract: Mobile GUI agents routinely encounter system permission dialogs during task execution, yet their ability to grant only permissions that are necessary for the delegated task remains largely unexamined. We present a systematic study of this capability, which we term Permission Literacy. We construct a four-level permission framework based on task relevance and privacy risk and validate the evaluated scenarios with three independent experts in GUI-agent safety. We inject Android-style permission popups into real GUI tasks and evaluate four frontier multimodal large language models using synchronized annotated screenshots and UI-tree hierarchies, making the requester, permission, justification, and available actions accessible to the agent. Beyond the main study, we conduct controlled interventions that separately vary task context and agent-visible requester identity. Under the same Calendar task, changing only the requester from Calendar to PiMusic reduces grants from 26/32 to 0/32, revealing a strong but task-conditioned App-Trust Bias. Holding a popup fixed while changing task context also substantially changes authorization decisions, revealing a systematic Task-Prior Override. Prompt interventions can reduce unnecessary grants, but their effectiveness is inconsistent across models and may come at the cost of suppressing legitimate grants. These results suggest that separating task execution from permission authorization is a promising design direction for future work.

</details>


### [217] [PURPOSE: Poisoning Conflict Resolution in RAG via Proxy-Fact-Grounded Updates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04756)
*Zijian Wang,Yubo Zhu,Muzhi Dong,Yanjun Lou,Yisheng Li,ZiLiang Zhang,Wei Tong,Yuan Zhang,Jingyu Hua,Sheng Zhong*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出PURPOSE，一种针对检索增强生成（RAG）中冲突消解机制的黑盒投毒攻击方法。与现有方法正面反驳冲突消解器不同，PURPOSE将注入重新定义为"最小化冲突的更新"，通过提取与查询相关的事实来锚定枢轴事件，使注入内容与消解器可能验证的信息一致，同时引导生成器输出目标答案。在3个QA基准、5个生成器和3种冲突消解方法上，PURPOSE在45个设置中的35个达到最高攻击成功率，平均ASR比最强基线高9.7个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RAG系统的后检索冲突消解机制是抵御嘈杂或矛盾检索段落的安全保障，但这一机制对知识投毒攻击的鲁棒性尚未得到充分研究。现有黑盒投毒方法均以正面矛盾方式断言目标答案，而这恰恰是冲突消解器设计用来检测的信号，导致攻击容易被识别和拦截。

Method: PURPOSE是一种严格黑盒投毒攻击方法，其核心思路是将注入从"反诉求"重新框定为"最小化冲突的更新"。具体而言，PURPOSE首先提取与查询相关的事实以近似消解器可能的参考信息，然后基于这些事实锚定一个枢轴事件，使注入内容与消解器可能验证的信息保持一致，同时将生成器引导向目标答案。

Result: 在3个QA基准、5个生成器和3种冲突消解方法的广泛实验中，PURPOSE在45个设置中的35个取得了最高攻击成功率（ASR），并且平均ASR比最强现有攻击方法高出9.7个百分点。

Conclusion: 实验结果证明所提出的投毒方法能有效对抗RAG中的冲突消解机制，并识别出"非矛盾性注入"作为增强投毒攻击的一种实用模式，揭示了现有冲突消解机制在应对此类隐蔽攻击时的脆弱性。

Abstract: In Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG), post-retrieval conflict resolution arbitrates among noisy or contradictory retrieved passages. However, the robustness of this safeguard against knowledge poisoning has not been adequately studied. Existing black-box poisoning methods all assert the target answer in frontal contradiction with what the resolver treats as settled, the very signal these methods are built to detect. We propose PURPOSE, a strict black-box poisoning attack that reframes the injection as an update that minimizes conflict, rather than as a counter-claim. PURPOSE extracts query-related facts approximating the resolver's possible reference, then grounds a pivot event in them to keep the injection consistent with what the resolver might verify while steering the generator toward the target answer. Across three QA benchmarks, five generators, and three conflict-resolution methods, PURPOSE attains the highest attack success rate (ASR) in 35 of 45 settings and exceeds the strongest prior attack with +9.7 mean ASR points. These results show that our poisoning method is effective against conflict resolution in RAG and identify non-contradicting injection as a practical mode to enhance poisoning attack.

</details>


### [218] [HoRFFI: High-Openness RF Fingerprint Identification with a Similarity-Enhanced Variational Information Bottleneck](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04881)
*Shuiguang Zeng,Yuxiang Shen,Yuanyu Zhang,Yulong Shen,Zhiyuan Tan,Houbing Herbert Song*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出HoRFFI框架，解决高开放度射频指纹识别（RFFI）问题，仅需少量标注训练设备即可实现可扩展的设备识别与未知设备拒绝。通过基于相似性增强的变分信息瓶颈（SVIB）监督机制，降低编码器对训练类别多样性的依赖，学习更具迁移性的嵌入空间。在LoRa和Wi-Fi数据集上，分别取得了0.112和0.288的Novel类准确率绝对提升，以及0.029和0.060的AUC绝对提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际RFFI系统需要注册新授权设备同时拒绝未知设备，但现有的开放集识别方法通常依赖大量且多样化的已知设备类别学习特征空间，这在仅有少量标注基类设备的实际场景中难以适用。因此提出了面向高开放度场景的RFFI框架。

Method: 提出HoRFFI框架，核心是相似性增强变分信息瓶颈（SVIB）监督机制。该机制通过特征空间增强和聚类推导样本间相似性信息，为嵌入空间的正则化提供补充监督，从而减少编码器对训练类别多样性的依赖。

Result: 在公共LoRa和Wi-Fi数据集上，HoRFFI在Novel类准确率上分别比最优基线提升0.112和0.288，在AUC上分别提升0.029和0.060。

Conclusion: HoRFFI有效解决了高开放度RFFI问题，在少量训练设备的条件下实现了可扩展的设备识别与未知设备拒绝，验证了SVIB监督机制的有效性及其嵌入空间的迁移能力。

Abstract: Radio frequency fingerprint identification (RFFI) is a promising technique for wireless device authentication. However, practical RFFI systems must enroll newly authorized devices while rejecting previously unseen ones, even when the feature extractor is trained on only a few labeled base-device classes, giving rise to a high-openness RFFI problem. Existing open-set recognition methods typically rely on feature spaces learned from a large and diverse set of known-device classes, limiting their applicability in practical scenarios. To address this challenge, we propose HoRFFI, a high-openness RFFI framework that supports scalable device identification and unknown-device rejection using only a small number of labeled training devices. HoRFFI employs a similarity-enhanced variational information bottleneck (SVIB)-based supervision mechanism, which reduces the encoder's dependence on training-class diversity and learns a more transferable embedding space. This supervision mechanism uses feature-space augmentation and clustering to derive inter-sample similarity information, which provides supplementary supervision for regularizing the embedding space. Experiments on public LoRa and Wi-Fi datasets show that HoRFFI achieves absolute improvements of \(0.112\) and \(0.288\) in novel-class accuracy, respectively, and corresponding absolute AUC improvements of \(0.029\) and \(0.060\) over the best-performing baselines.

</details>


### [219] [When Does Latent Communication Pay? A Causal Audit of Relayed KV Caches in Multi-Agent LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04893)
*Jiaming Cheng,Subhransu Das,Rajiv Ramnath*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文通过因果审计（deranged-cache audit）检验多智能体LLM系统中继的关键-值缓存是否真正传递"潜在思想"（即特定示例的配对效应），而非仅仅产生缓存效应。研究发现，当接收方需要发送方的私有信息时，不匹配缓存导致性能从天花板跌至23-25%；当不需要时，不匹配缓存与匹配缓存在统计等价范围内（2.8分以内）。结果表明大缓存效应不一定等于配对效应，基准测试分数差异本身不足以证明潜在思想传输。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体LLM系统通过中继关键-值缓存（KV cache）而非文本进行通信，其性能增益被归因于"潜在思想"的交换。但这一归因隐含了一个更强的声明：具体是中继了哪一个示例的缓存（配对效应），而不仅仅是中继了某种缓存。现有研究缺乏对这种因果关系的严格验证。

Method: 对已发布的系统进行因果审计，将中继的缓存替换为：混乱不匹配示例的缓存、零化缓存、以及矩匹配随机缓存。在两个条件下进行测试：（1）接收方需要发送方的私有信息；（2）不需要。使用预注册的五种子协议、Holm校正的TOST等价检验，覆盖三个家族、五个检查点及多种基准（GSM8K、ARC-Challenge、MedQA），评估Qwen3多个规模和多种传输通道（LatentMAS、KVComm、C2C）。

Result: 在需要私有信息的场景下，不匹配缓存导致性能从100%跌至23-25%（对比答案无关中继），该对比在三个家族、五个检查点和文档QA任务上一致复现。在不需要私有信息的场景下，等价检验显示不匹配与匹配缓存的差异在2.8分以内（该界限锚定于被审计系统的报告增益）；一个单元格在界限内检测到小的优势。另外发现零化中继缓存损失14.7分，但不匹配缓存仅损失0.4分。不同传输通道差异显著：LatentMAS原生中继达到天花板，KVComm层子集仅部分传递，C2C的投影器无检测到的示例特定传输。

Conclusion: 基准测试的分数差异本身不能证明潜在思想传输的真实性；必须通过不匹配缓存审计来建立这一结论。作者发布了该审计工具供社区使用。

Abstract: Multi-agent LLM systems relay key--value caches instead of text and credit their gains to exchanged ``latent thoughts''. That credit is a claim about \emph{which} example's cache is relayed, not merely that one is. We audit it causally in released systems. The cache is replaced with deranged (mismatched-example), zeroed, and moment-matched random counterparts, under two regimes defined by whether the receiver needs the sender's private information. Where it does, the battery reads ceiling: 100\% against 23--25\% for answer-irrelevant relays on the primary backbone, a contrast replicated across three families, five checkpoints, and a prose document-QA surface. Where it does not, a pre-registered five-seed protocol establishes equivalence within 2.8 points, a margin anchored to the audited system's reported gain, under Holm-corrected TOST on GSM8K and ARC-Challenge across three Qwen3 scales and on MedQA at 8B (one cell shows a small detected advantage inside the margin); a second family shows no detected advantage. A large cache effect need not be a pairing effect. In one natural cell, zeroing the relay costs 14.7 points; a mismatched cache, 0.4. Nor is need sufficient: under the same test, delivered channels span ceiling (LatentMAS's native relay), partial (KVComm's layer subset), and no detected example-specific transfer (C2C's released projector). Benchmark deltas do not by themselves establish latent-thought transmission; establishing it takes a mismatched-cache audit, which we release.

</details>


### [220] [LLM-Assisted Detection and Repair of Hardware Security Vulnerabilities in Verilog Designs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04907)
*Ethen Santana,Gabriel Gyaase,Hao Zheng*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种利用大语言模型（LLM）直接从Verilog硬件设计中识别潜在硬件常见弱点枚举（CWE）的方法，并在单模块Verilog设计数据集上进行了迭代评估，证明了LLM在硬件安全分析中提供自动化、可扩展辅助的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 硬件设计像软件一样容易存在引入安全漏洞的缺陷，但与软件不同，硬件缺陷在制造后永久嵌入硅片中，难以或无法修补。因此需要在设计阶段尽早检测这些安全弱点（如访问控制不当、敏感信息泄露、权限提升等），以提高硬件安全性。

Method: 提出一种利用大语言模型（LLM）直接从Verilog硬件设计中识别潜在硬件CWE的方法论，并在单模块Verilog设计数据集上进行迭代评估以检验其检测硬件安全弱点的有效性。

Result: 实验结果表明，LLM在从Verilog设计中检测硬件安全弱点方面具有潜力，能够为传统硬件安全分析提供自动化、可扩展的辅助能力。

Conclusion: LLM可以作为一种有效的工具，在硬件设计过程中提供自动化的安全漏洞检测辅助，弥补传统硬件安全分析方法在可扩展性和自动化方面的不足。

Abstract: Hardware designs, like software, are susceptible to bugs that can introduce security vulnerabilities and create opportunities for malicious exploitation. Unlike software vulnerabilities, however, hardware flaws become permanently embedded in silicon after fabrication, making them difficult or impossible to patch. Many of these weaknesses are categorized under the Common Weakness Enumeration (CWE) framework and include improper access control, exposure of sensitive information, and unintended privilege escalation. To improve the detection of such vulnerabilities, we propose a methodology that leverages a Large Language Model (LLM) to identify potential hardware CWEs directly from hardware designs in Verilog. The proposed approach is evaluated iteratively on a dataset of single-module Verilog designs to assess its effectiveness in detecting hardware security weaknesses. Our results demonstrate the potential of LLMs to augment traditional hardware security analysis by providing automated, scalable assistance for identifying security vulnerabilities during the hardware design process.

</details>


### [221] [Toward Practical Decentralized Proof-of-Location via Physical Witnessing Zones](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04957)
*Tamor Tomson,Eduardo Brito,Amnir Hadachi,Ulrich Norbisrath*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文基于去中心化位置证明（PoL）的理论研究，使用低成本硬件构建了物理原型系统。通过构建见证区域，固定邻近设备测量证明者的存在、通过本地网状网络交换声明并记录在防篡改账本中。受控室内实验表明系统能生成准确、低延迟的证明对象，同时检测重放和恶意测距攻击，为下一代数字信任基础设施提供了可复用的实验基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统位置声明依赖于自报数据，容易伪造且难以事后验证。数字服务日益需要可靠的位置证明机制，而现有PoL系统多停留在理论或仿真层面，缺乏实际硬件实现来验证其在真实条件下的可行性。

Method: 将抽象的去中心化PoL协议适配到物理约束条件下，实现了三个关键适配：见证者发起的测距（witness-initiated ranging）、跨见证者一致性检查（cross-witness consistency checks）、以及针对重放攻击的新鲜度绑定（freshness binding against replay）。原型系统由低成本硬件构成，固定设备组成见证区域，通过本地网状网络交换位置声明并记录于防篡改账本中。

Result: 受控室内评估显示系统能够产生准确且低延迟的证明对象，并成功检测模拟的重放攻击和恶意测距攻击，证明了从理论仿真到物理原型的可行性。

Conclusion: 该原型为去中心化位置证据提供了可复用的实验基线，同时揭示了剩余的校准、验证者独立性、无线电完整性和扩展性等关键需求，需要在真实无线电、网络和时序条件下进一步验证和改进。

Abstract: Digital services increasingly rely on claims that a person, device, or asset was in a specific place at a specific time. Today, those claims often depend on self-reported location data, which is easy to falsify and difficult to verify after the fact. Proof-of-Location (PoL) systems address this gap by turning presence claims into evidence that an independent verifier can later inspect. This paper builds upon recent theoretical work on decentralized PoL architectures and demonstrates how they can move from emulation to a physical prototype built with low-cost hardware. We implement a witnessing zone in which fixed nearby devices measure a prover's presence, exchange claims over a local mesh, and record them in a tamper-evident ledger. Building the prototype required adapting the abstract protocol to physical constraints through witness-initiated ranging, cross-witness consistency checks, and freshness binding against replay. Our controlled indoor evaluation shows that the system can produce accurate, low-latency proof objects while detecting simulated replay and malicious-ranging attacks. The result is a reusable experimental baseline for next-generation digital trust infrastructure, that exposes the remaining calibration, verifier-independence, radio-integrity, and scaling requirements for decentralized location evidence, where physical presence claims can be independently checked under real radio, networking, and timing conditions.

</details>


### [222] [When Do PEFT Adaptations Leak Structure? Measuring Black-Box Structural Bounds in Public-Base Model Services](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05036)
*Zhongjiang Yao,Shuangshuang Liang,Chun Yang,LiWei Chen,Gang Shi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出VectorHijack-SR测量方法，通过配对受害者/基础模型的残差分析，将PEFT服务的结构信息（家族、层定位、秩）转化为校准边界。实验表明在多个分类骨干网络上存在结构泄漏，但存在可见性-可利用性差距：结构信息可泄漏，而两阶段恢复和生成攻击效果有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 服务商越来越多地部署带有私有参数高效微调（PEFT）的公开基础模型，当审计者或攻击者可以在本地执行公开基础模型并观察受害者输出时，会产生差异化信息泄漏风险。现有工作缺乏对这一风险的系统性测量方法。

Method: 提出VectorHijack-SR测量方法，将成对的受害者/基础模型残差转换为PEFT家族、层定位和粗粒度秩的校准结构边界。聚合查询级多种统计特征（幅度、排名、熵、边际、长度、模板、定位、频谱），通过服务不交叉分类器量化结构证据，并用交叉拟合分层拒绝器评估受害者是否在校准的LoRA流形之外。

Result: 家族泄漏在BERT/MNLI（8/12）、RoBERTa/MNLI（21/24）、DeBERTa-v3/MNLI（12/18）和AG News（15/18）上超过均匀随机水平。秩推断依赖任务：BERT/MNLI和DeBERTa/AG News达到8/9，而DeBERTa/MNLI仅4/9且与随机一致。十种子BERT开集网格上拒绝器AUROC为0.804，已知准确率0.956，但在结构相近的DoRA和LoRA+head变体上受限。五个LoRA-r64服务的精确版本链接AUC达0.940。两阶段恢复无公平预算查询节省，后验选择PEFT不如蒸馏-转换PEFT（0.356 vs 0.517）。

Conclusion: 已知基础模型、丰富输出的PEFT服务可能泄漏可操作的结构和版本信息，但仅凭闭集置信度不能建立通用的适配器恢复能力。存在可见性-可利用性差距，需要更全面的风险评估框架。

Abstract: Services increasingly deploy public foundation models with private parameter-efficient adaptations, creating a differential information leakage risk when auditors or adversaries can execute the public base model locally and observe victim outputs. We present VectorHijack-SR, a measurement methodology that converts paired victim/base residuals into calibrated structural bounds over PEFT family, layer locality, and coarse rank, while separating metadata visibility from open-world validity and operational exploitability. Our estimator aggregates query-level magnitude, ranking, entropy, margin, length, template, locality, and spectral statistics into service-level representations. A service-disjoint classifier quantifies structural evidence, and a cross-fitted hierarchical rejector evaluates whether a victim lies outside the calibrated LoRA manifold. Across classification backbones, family leakage exceeds uniform chance on BERT/MNLI (8/12), RoBERTa/MNLI (21/24), and DeBERTa-v3 on MNLI (12/18) and AG News (15/18). Rank inference is task dependent: BERT/MNLI and DeBERTa/AG News reach 8/9, whereas DeBERTa/MNLI achieves 4/9 and is statistically compatible with chance after correction. On a ten-seed BERT open-set grid, the rejector achieves pooled AUROC 0.804 (95% CI [0.660, 0.927]) and known accuracy 0.956, but remains limited on structurally close DoRA and LoRA+head variants. Exact-version linkage on five held-out LoRA-r64 services reaches AUC 0.940. Experiments reveal a visibility-exploitability gap: two-stage recovery provides no fair-budget query savings, posterior-selected PEFT underperforms distill-then-convert PEFT (0.356 vs. 0.517), and free-running generation remains near chance. These results show that known-base, rich-output PEFT services can leak actionable structural and version information, while closed-set confidence alone does not establish universal adapter recovery.

</details>


### [223] [Private Direct Preference Optimization for LLM Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05040)
*Yangfan Jiang,Fei Wei,Ergute Bao,Xiaokui Xiao,Yaliang Li,Bolin Ding*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了偏好隐私（preference privacy）这一新的隐私概念，针对DPO中仅需保护响应间相对偏好信息的场景，设计了PrivDPO方法，通过在偏好轴上施加校准随机扰动来实现隐私保护，且与大规模LLM训练兼容。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DPO已成为LLM对齐的标准方法，但其人类偏好数据中的相对偏好反映主观判断，可能泄露标注者或最终用户的敏感属性。现成的隐私保护方法与该数据结构的匹配度不高，会导致不必要的噪声注入和有偏更新。

Method: 形式化定义偏好隐私（label-DP风格的隐私概念），仅保护候选响应间的相对偏好。关键观察是：相邻样本仅偏好信号不同时，梯度差异位于由文本决定的单一偏好轴上。PrivDPO通过无偏随机重缩放DPO目标，只沿该轴添加校准随机性，避免逐样本梯度操作。

Result: 在三个对齐基准和三个LLM家族上的实验表明，与隐私保护基线相比，PrivDPO始终实现了更好的隐私-效用权衡。

Conclusion: PrivDPO在保护偏好隐私的同时保持了良好的模型性能，为DPO场景下的隐私保护提供了一种高效且有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Direct preference optimization (DPO) is now a standard method for aligning large language models (LLMs) using human preference data. Each DPO example contains a prompt and a pair of candidate model responses. While prompts and responses are often public or model-generated, the relative preference between responses reflects subjective judgments and can reveal sensitive attributes of annotators or end users. Off-the-shelf privacy-preserving approaches are not well matched to this structure, leading to unnecessary noise injection and biased updates in training.
  In this paper, we formalize preference privacy, a label-DP-style privacy notion for DPO that protects only the relative preference between candidate responses, assuming an adversary who already knows the prompt and responses. We then design PrivDPO, a DPO variant that enforces preference privacy while remaining compatible with large-scale LLM training. Our main observation is that, for neighboring examples differing only in their preference signal, the gradient difference lies on a one-dimensional preference axis determined solely by the text; all preference information flows through this axis. PrivDPO adds calibrated randomness only along this axis via an unbiased randomized rescaling of the DPO objective, avoiding per-example gradient operations. Our experiments on three alignment benchmarks and three LLM families show that PrivDPO consistently achieves strong privacy-utility trade-offs compared with privacy-preserving baselines.

</details>


### [224] [Gradient Immunity: Null-Space Resistance to Malicious Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05045)
*Yuxuan Huang,Xingyu Zeng,Tianhang Zheng,Chaochao Lu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种单向安全门（USG）机制，通过空空间立方层和逆适配器在发布时保护开放权重模型的关键组件，使下游微调时恶意样本的梯度被阻断，从而维持攻击成功率接近发布前水平，同时不牺牲正常样本的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的大语言模型安全防御主要针对微调即服务（FTaaS）范式或依赖下游用户遵循额外安全程序，无法直接解决提供商在部分受保护开放权重（PPOW）发布场景中面临的威胁——即大部分权重可训练，仅有少量安全关键组件在发布时被保留的问题。

Method: 提出Unidirectional Safety Gate（USG），包含一个Null Space Cubic Layer和一个Inverse Adapter，插入在最终Transformer层之后。立方层在下游微调时对落入校准保护区域（由防御者持有的恶意数据校准阈值）的恶意样本隐藏状态进行梯度抑制或阻断，而逆适配器恢复基础模型的前向行为，保证正常推理不受影响。

Result: 在六个模型-数据集设置中，USG在固定发布阈值下将微调后攻击成功率保持在接近发布前水平，同时在较简单设置中维持高安全通过率，在BeaverTails不安全样本上展现出更清晰的安全-效用权衡。

Conclusion: 发布时表示空间阻断可以有效提高恶意下游适配的成本，且不需要下游用户配合，为开放权重模型的安全发布提供了一种新的防御思路。

Abstract: Released aligned large language models remain vulnerable to malicious downstream finetuning. Existing defenses are largely designed for the fine-tuning-as-a-service (FTaaS) paradigm or rely on downstream users to follow additional safety procedures, and therefore do not directly address the setting we study: a provider controlled partially protected open-weight (PPOW) release setting in which most weights remain trainable while a small safety-critical component is preserved at release. We propose a Unidirectional Safety Gate (USG), instantiated as a Null Space Cubic Layer together with an Inverse Adapter inserted after the final Transformer layer. During downstream fine-tuning, the cubic layer suppresses or blocks gradients from harmful samples whose hidden states fall in a calibrated protected region, while the Inverse Adapter restores the base model's forward behavior. In practice, we calibrate a threshold using defender-held harmful data, allowing protection to generalize to nearby in-distribution harmful samples. Across six evaluated model-dataset settings, USG keeps post-finetuning attack success rate close to the pre-release level under a fixed release threshold, while maintaining high safe-pass rates on easier settings and exhibiting a clearer safety-utility trade-off on unsafe samples from BeaverTails. These results suggest that release-time representation-space blocking can raise the cost of malicious downstream adaptation without requiring downstream cooperation. The code is available at https://github.com/OpenCausaLab/Gradient-Immunity.

</details>


### [225] [Kerckhoffs-Compliant Watermarking for Physical Design IP Protection: From Placement to Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05055)
*Andrew B. Kahng,Yiting Liu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: PDMarks是一个Kerckhoffs合规的物理设计水印框架，通过32字节密钥经HMAC-SHA256确定性生成水印实例，在布局、时钟树综合和布线多个阶段嵌入所有权证据，实验证明其在保持较小PPA开销的同时提供极强（<10^{-32}）的所有权证明，并集成于OpenROAD-flow-scripts。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的物理设计水印方法要么只保护单一PD阶段，要么依赖隐藏的构造细节，在面对白盒攻击者时存在脆弱性。随着PD工具访问范围的扩大，对已布局布线的数据库进行未经授权的重用日益成为问题，需要一种安全性仅依赖于密钥的安全水印方案。

Method: PDMarks使用HMAC-SHA256从32字节秘密密钥确定性推导所有水印实例和目标值，将所有权证据嵌入PD流程的多个阶段（布局、时钟树综合和布线）。该框架遵循Kerckhoffs原则，安全仅依赖密钥保密。已集成到OpenROAD-flow-scripts中。

Result: 在NanGate45和ASAP7设计上的实验表明，PDMarks相比先前物理设计水印方法提供更强的所有权证据，同时PPA开销相当或更小。所有评估设计的近似联合全阶段重合概率均低于10^{-32}。错误密钥无法复现完整所有权证明，削弱水印需要广泛扰动受保护的实现。

Conclusion: PDMarks通过多阶段水印嵌入和密钥确定性推导机制，提供了安全、稳健且高效的物理设计IP保护方案，有效抵御白盒攻击者，是现有PD水印方法的重要改进。

Abstract: Physical design (PD) intellectual property (IP) is a valuable artifact of modern VLSI implementation. It includes optimized cell placement, clock distribution, and routing decisions produced by carefully tuned PD flows. As access to PD tools expands, unauthorized reuse of placed-and-routed databases becomes an increasing concern. Existing PD watermarking methods either protect only one PD stage or rely on hidden construction details, leaving them vulnerable to a white-box adversary. In this work, we develop PDMarks, a Kerckhoffs-compliant watermarking framework whose security depends only on a secret key. PDMarks embeds ownership evidence across multiple stages of the PD flow, including placement, clock tree synthesis (CTS), and routing. All watermark instances and target values are deterministically derived from a 32-byte secret key using HMAC-SHA256, enabling consistent embedding and verification. PDMarks has been integrated into OpenROAD-flow-scripts. Experiments on NanGate45 and ASAP7 designs show that PDMarks outperforms prior physical design watermarking methods by providing much stronger ownership evidence with comparable or smaller PPA overhead. The approximate joint all-stage coincidence probability is below 10^{-32} for every evaluated design. Wrong-key and attack evaluations further show that incorrect keys do not reproduce the complete ownership proof and that weakening the watermark requires broad perturbation of the protected implementation.

</details>


### [226] [Hardware Design and Security in the Era of Chiplets and LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05063)
*Johann Knechtel,Ozgur Sinanoglu,Paul V. Gratz,Ramesh Karri*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文对半导体行业两大前沿趋势——异构2.5D芯片组系统与大语言模型（LLM）辅助电子设计自动化（EDA）流程——进行了统一的安全分析，涵盖从体系结构、逻辑到物理层面的硬件攻击面扩展，并综述了相应的防御方案，包括2.5D分割制造与有源中介层实现的物理隔离信任根架构，以及LLM驱动EDA流水线的威胁识别与防御技术。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 半导体行业正经历双重变革：向异构2.5D芯片组系统迁移以及将LLM集成到EDA流程中。虽然这些技术范式在良率、模块化、设计生产率等方面带来前所未有的收益，但也极大地扩展了硬件攻击面，目前缺乏对这些新型安全挑战的系统性统一分析。

Method: 本文采用统一分析框架，分别从两个维度展开：一是对芯片组系统（包括用于LLM加速的硬件堆栈）在体系结构、逻辑和物理层面的攻击进行系统梳理；二是对LLM驱动的EDA流水线中的各类攻击与防御技术进行综述，并进一步讨论LLM系统如何反向助力现代系统的硬件安全。

Result: 综述了针对芯片组系统的多层次攻击类型及基于2.5D分割制造和有源中介层的可信根防御架构；识别了LLM驱动EDA流水线的原生安全威胁并回顾了最新防御技术；同时探讨了LLM在推进芯片组等现代系统硬件安全中的积极作用。

Conclusion: 本文为芯片组系统与LLM驱动EDA流程提供了统一的安全视角，表明在享受双重技术革命带来的收益时，必须同步建立系统化的安全防护体系，且LLM本身也可成为强化硬件安全的工具。

Abstract: The semiconductor industry is undergoing a dual revolution: the shift toward heterogeneous 2.5D chiplet systems and the integration of Large Language Models (LLMs) into Electronic Design Automation (EDA) flows. While these paradigms offer unprecedented benefits in yield, modularity, design productivity, etc., they radically expand the hardware attack surface. This paper provides a unified analysis of these frontiers, ranging from attacks on chiplet systems (including hardware stacks for LLM acceleration) across architectural, logical, and physical levels, to various exploits against LLM-driven EDA pipelines. To secure chiplet systems, we review a powerful defense approach that leverages 2.5D split manufacturing and active interposers for physically isolated Root of Trust (RoT) architectures. To secure LLM-driven EDA pipelines, we first identify native threats and then review state-of-the-art defense techniques. Finally, we discuss how LLM systems can advance hardware security efforts for modern systems, including chiplets.

</details>


### [227] [Agent Against Agent: An Agentic System for Automatic Prompt Injection Red Teaming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05108)
*Yanting Wang,Chenlong Yin,Runpeng Geng,Jinyuan Jia*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出PIMiner，一个用于提示注入红队测试的智能体系统，通过训练序列化（数据集、目标模型）对构建策略库，可在测试时直接迁移到未见过的目标LLM，无需额外训练，且仅需少量查询即可实现高攻击成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提示注入对LLM智能体构成严重安全风险，而现有基于强化学习的红队测试方法生成的攻击模型往往难以泛化到新的目标LLM，需要更高效且可迁移的提示注入红队测试方法。

Method: PIMiner采用智能体系统架构，训练阶段在序列化的（数据集、目标模型）对上学习并构建策略库，测试阶段将学到的策略库直接迁移到未见过的目标LLM上，无需额外训练，每个测试样本仅需约10次查询。

Result: 在IPIArena上，PIMiner对Gemini-2.5-Pro达到76.2% ASR，对GPT-5.1达到61.9% ASR，对Claude-Sonnet-4.5达到42.9% ASR；在AgentDojo上，分别达到86.7%、53.3%和40.0%的ASR。

Conclusion: PIMiner展示了强大的跨模型泛化能力，仅需少量查询即可在多个主流LLM上实现较高的提示注入攻击成功率，为LLM智能体的安全评估和防御数据收集提供了有效工具。

Abstract: Prompt injection poses significant security risks to LLM agents. Efficient and effective red-teaming is therefore critical, both for evaluating these risks and for collecting training data to improve defenses. Existing state-of-the-art prompt injection red-teaming methods primarily rely on reinforcement learning (RL), producing attacker models that often generalize poorly to new target LLMs. In this work, we develop PIMiner, an agentic system for prompt injection red-teaming. During training, PIMiner is trained on a sequence of (dataset, target model) pairs and builds a strategy library from scratch. At test time, the learned strategy library can be directly transferred to a previously unseen target LLM without additional training. PIMiner requires only a small number of queries to a target agent (e.g., 10) per test sample. Experimental results demonstrate that PIMiner achieves strong performance. On IPIArena, it attains a 76.2% ASR against Gemini-2.5-Pro, 61.9% ASR against GPT-5.1, and 42.9% ASR against Claude-Sonnet-4.5. On AgentDojo, it achieves an 86.7% ASR against Gemini-2.5-Pro, 53.3% ASR against GPT-5.1, and 40.0% ASR against Claude-Sonnet-4.5.

</details>


### [228] [Temporal Context Awareness: A Defense Framework Against Multi-turn Manipulation Attacks on Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.15560)
*Prashant Kulkarni,Assaf Namer*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出TCA（时间上下文感知）框架，一种针对大规模语言模型多轮对话操纵攻击的新型防御机制，通过分析语义漂移、跨轮意图一致性和对话模式演变来检测和缓解操纵行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模语言模型日益受到复杂的多轮对话操纵攻击的威胁，攻击者通过看似良性的对话逐步构建上下文以绕过安全措施。这类攻击利用对话的时间特性规避单轮检测方法，构成严重的安全漏洞，亟需上下文感知的防御方案。

Method: TCA框架集成三种核心能力：动态上下文嵌入分析、跨轮一致性验证和渐进式风险评分，以此连续监控对话中的语义漂移、意图一致性和演变中的对话模式，识别传统检测方法容易遗漏的微妙操纵模式。

Result: 在模拟对抗场景中的初步评估表明，TCA框架能够有效识别传统检测技术常遗漏的微妙操纵模式，展现了检测多轮对话操纵攻击的潜力。此外，论文还分析了多种攻击向量在多轮对话中的演变过程。

Conclusion: 研究强调对话AI系统迫切需要稳健的上下文感知防御机制，TCA框架为保护LLM安全同时保持其合法应用实用性提供了有前景的方向，代码已开源以支持后续研究。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly vulnerable to sophisticated multi-turn manipulation attacks, where adversaries strategically build context through seemingly benign conversational turns to circumvent safety measures and elicit harmful or unauthorized responses. These attacks exploit the temporal nature of dialogue to evade single-turn detection methods, representing a critical security vulnerability with significant implications for real-world deployments.
  This paper introduces the Temporal Context Awareness (TCA) framework, a novel defense mechanism designed to address this challenge by continuously analyzing semantic drift, cross-turn intention consistency and evolving conversational patterns. The TCA framework integrates dynamic context embedding analysis, cross-turn consistency verification, and progressive risk scoring to detect and mitigate manipulation attempts effectively. Preliminary evaluations on simulated adversarial scenarios demonstrate the framework's potential to identify subtle manipulation patterns often missed by traditional detection techniques, offering a much-needed layer of security for conversational AI systems. In addition to outlining the design of TCA , we analyze diverse attack vectors and their progression across multi-turn conversation, providing valuable insights into adversarial tactics and their impact on LLM vulnerabilities. Our findings underscore the pressing need for robust, context-aware defenses in conversational AI systems and highlight TCA framework as a promising direction for securing LLMs while preserving their utility in legitimate applications. We make our implementation available to support further research in this emerging area of AI security.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [229] [A Long-Run Persistence Theory for AI Systems under the Redundancy-Adjusted Artificial Age Score (AAS)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04012)
*Seyma Yaman Kayadibi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于冗余调整人工年龄评分（AAS）的AI系统长期持续性框架，将AAS从静态评估指标扩展为周期级泛函，证明AI系统可以在无限次循环操作中保持结构年龄有界，而非不可避免地累积劣化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI系统越来越多地需要在重复的交互、适应和更新循环中运行，而非孤立的单次输出。这引发了一个基本理论问题：AI系统能否无限期持续而不产生无界的结构老化？现有AAS只是静态评估指标，缺乏对长期循环运行中结构年龄演化规律的形式化分析框架。

Method: 本文将AAS从静态评估量扩展为周期级泛函，生成跨重复操作中的年龄序列。在每个周期，结构年龄通过加权且冗余感知的对数惩罚函数定义，基于组件一致性水平计算。在此基础上定义了渐进制度的分层体系（包括负担持续、零负担持续、振荡持续和累积终末负担），并建立了比较排序、敏感性界、组件稳定化下的收敛性、有限全变差下的持续性、阻尼跨周期扰动下的几何稳定化，以及非退化冗余条件下的零负担刻画。

Result: 主要结果是：无限循环延续不需要无界的结构老化。AI系统可以通过无限多个循环而结构年龄保持有界；在更强的正则条件下，边际老化趋近于零；在最强的制度下，周期级负担收敛为零。研究还建立了一系列关于敏感性、收敛性和稳定化的理论边界。

Conclusion: 该框架为分析AI长期持续性提供了形式化基础，将问题重新定义为有界结构负担而非不可避免的累积劣化，从而在理论上排除了点状老化的爆炸性增长。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence systems are increasingly expected to operate over repeated cycles of interaction, adaptation, and update rather than through isolated one-shot outputs. This raises a fundamental theoretical question: can an AI system persist indefinitely without incurring unbounded structural aging? This paper develops a long-run persistence framework for AI systems based on the redundancy-adjusted Artificial Age Score (AAS). The model extends AAS from a static evaluative measure into a cycle-level functional that generates an age sequence across repeated operation. At each cycle, structural age is defined through a weighted, redundancy-aware logarithmic penalty over component consistency levels. Within this framework, cycle-level age is shown to be well defined and uniformly bounded, thereby excluding explosive pointwise aging. On this basis, the paper defines a hierarchy of asymptotic regimes, including burdened persistence, zero-burden persistence, oscillatory persistence, and cumulative terminal burden. It also establishes comparative ordering, sensitivity bounds, convergence under componentwise stabilization, persistence under finite total variation, geometric stabilization under damped inter-cycle perturbations, and a zero-burden characterization under nondegenerate redundancy conditions. The main result is that indefinite cyclic continuation does not require unbounded structural aging: an AI system may pass through infinitely many cycles while its structural age remains bounded, while under stronger regularity conditions its marginal aging vanishes and, in the strongest regime, its cycle-level burden converges to zero. The framework thus provides a formal basis for analyzing long-run artificial persistence as a problem of bounded structural burden rather than inevitable cumulative deterioration.

</details>


### [230] [The LLM Proposes, the Executive Disposes: A Self-Verifying Agent Instrument that Dissociates Commitment Drift from Binding Drift in Long-Horizon Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04066)
*Mohsen Arjmandi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结构性验证代理（agent）的仪器化方法：确定性执行者拥有全部信念，语言模型仅能提交类型化提案，声明必须在行动前预先注册预测并由代码匹配观察后才被接纳。每次运行都会在写入错误、渲染尺寸或盐罐回显地板被突破时自我失效（8次运行中有4次被失效，每次定位到真实缺陷）；同时通过渲染不可见的影子参考在所有消融单元中编译完整系统本应提交的计划，从而在机制被移除的单元中也能定义漂移指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程代理自身的状态和自我报告不可信，传统的事后验证方法无法解决这一问题。作者希望构建一种结构性而非事后性的验证机制，使验证内嵌于代理架构本身，而非依赖事后检查。

Method: 提出代理仪器（agent instrument）架构：确定性执行者拥有所有信念，语言模型只能提交类型化提案，声明只有在行动前预先注册的预测被代码与观察匹配后才被接纳。通过写入错误、渲染尺寸、盐罐回显等自失效机制确保每次运行的有效性；使用渲染不可见的影子参考在所有消融单元中编译完整系统的计划，使漂移指标在机制被移除的单元中仍可定义。

Result: 消融承诺机制使目标放弃率从0.00翻转至1.00，而绑定错误保持0.00（每个单元三种子，每次运行最多394个参考节拍，所有运行门控有效）。绑定通道的修复被消融时，其漂移不会以节拍级漂移重新出现，因为绑定由代码拥有，失败类别被结构性吸收，仅在上一层以假设形成崩溃的形式残留。任务效能为零（52次门控运行在ARC-AGI-3上零级完成），作为预注册的结构性推翻条件全面披露。

Conclusion: 贡献在于为代理开发提供了一种验证方法论，以及其使可测量的漂移分解。该仪器验证的是自身的科学性，而非仅验证代理本身——每次运行能在缺陷出现时自失效并定位真实缺陷，漂移指标在机制移除后仍可定义。

Abstract: How do you verify a long-horizon agent when its own state and self-reports are exactly what you cannot trust? We present an agent instrument built so that verification is structural rather than post-hoc. A deterministic Executive owns all belief; a language model may only file typed proposals, and a claim is admitted only when a prediction pre-registered before acting is matched against observation by code. Two properties make the instrument a verifier of its own science, not just of the agent: every run invalidates itself when per-organ write-error, render-size, or salted-canary-echo floors are breached (four of the first eight architecture runs were invalidated, each localizing a real defect); and a render-invisible shadow reference compiles the plan the full system would have committed in every ablation cell, so drift metrics are defined even where the mechanism under test has been removed. Using this instrument we report a clean, single-variable result on a failure every long-horizon agent suffers: ablating the commitment mechanism flips goal-abandonment from 0.00 to 1.00 while binding error stays flat at 0.00 (three seeds per cell, up to 394 reference beats per run, every run gated valid). The binding channel, by contrast, does not reappear as per-beat drift when its repair is ablated -- because binding is code-owned, the failure class is structurally absorbed, its only residue appearing one layer upstream as a collapse in hypothesis formation. We report these under full disclosure that task efficacy is null (zero level completions across 52 gated runs on ARC-AGI-3), pre-registered as a structural defeater. The contribution is a verification methodology for agent development and the drift decomposition it makes measurable.

</details>


### [231] [Monte Carlo Tree Search for Table-to-Multimodal Report Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04071)
*Teng Lin,Zhiyang Zhang,Yuyu Luo,Nan Tang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MCTS-Report框架，利用蒙特卡洛树搜索将多模态表格到报告生成建模为渐进式构建过程，通过原子动作分解、多维度奖励函数和思维轨迹存储，在MMRBench基准上达到77.9综合评分，显著优于现有基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法依赖固定线性流程和孤立的子任务处理，无法联合优化报告生成中的事实准确性、视觉质量和叙事连贯性，亟需一种能够协同优化各环节的统一框架。

Method: 提出MCTS-Report框架：(1) 将报告生成分解为章节规划、可视化任务识别、图表生成、洞察组织和叙事润色等原子动作；(2) 利用MCTS在结构化搜索空间中逐步构建报告，LLM负责动态推理和动作执行；(3) 在树节点中存储推理轨迹以实现上下文感知的连贯构建；(4) 设计多维度奖励函数，涵盖SQL数值一致性检查、图表质量、图文对齐、结构完整性，并加入多样性惩罚和前置条件剪枝；(5) 构建MMRBench基准，包含六个领域的真实表格及专家精修参考结构。

Result: 在MMRBench上的实验表明，MCTS-Report在结构完整性、数值准确性、图文对齐和洞察新颖性等维度均显著优于强基线方法，综合得分达到77.9。

Conclusion: MCTS驱动的渐进式构建方法能够有效统一多模态报告生成中各子任务的优化目标，并通过搜索空间探索和奖励引导实现高质量报告生成，为数据智能领域的自动化报告生成提供了新范式。

Abstract: Automatically generating professional multimodal reports comprising both textual analysis and visual charts from structured tabular data is a critical challenge in data intelligence. Existing methods suffer from fixed linear pipelines and isolated subtask processing, which hinder joint optimization of factual accuracy, visual quality, and narrative coherence. To address these issues, this paper proposes MCTS-Report, a Monte Carlo Tree Search (MCTS)-driven framework that formulates multimodal table-to-report generation as a progressive construction process over a structured search space. The core idea is to decompose report generation into atomic actions, including chapter planning, visualization task identification, chart generation, insight organization, and narrative refinement, each executed by an LLM based on dynamic reasoning conditioned on the current report state. We use an LLM to generate step-by-step reasoning and actions during MCTS, storing the reasoning trajectory in each node for context-aware, coherent report construction. To guide the search, we design a multi-dimensional reward function that jointly evaluates numerical fact consistency (via SQL), chart quality, chart-text alignment, and structural completeness, while incorporating a diversity penalty to suppress repeated charts and a precondition check to prune invalid actions. We also construct MMRBench, a comprehensive benchmark comprising real-world tables from six domains, paired with expert-refined reference report structures and verifiable key insights. Experiments on MMRBench demonstrate that MCTS-Report significantly outperforms strong baselines across structural completeness, numerical accuracy, chart-text alignment, and insight novelty, achieving a 77.9 overall score.

</details>


### [232] [FinProBench: Evaluating Financial AI Agents with Role-Grounded Rubrics Derived from Professional Deliverables](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04077)
*Ben Wang,Kang Zhou,Lifan Guo,Feng Chen,Chi Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了金融AI智能体评估的新方法，引入FinProBench基准和角色接地规则构建（RGRC）流水线，通过从业者的实际交付物提取隐性标准来构建评分规则，弥补了传统基于提示词或模型输出的规则构建方法忽视实务隐性标准的不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有金融AI智能体评估规则通常从任务提示或模型输出中提取标准，忽略了只有从业者实际交付物中才能体现的隐性专业标准。需要一种与真实专业工作对齐的评估框架。

Method: 提出RGRC四阶段流水线：交付物收集（Deliverable Collection）、能力提取（Competency Extraction）、规则合成（Rubric Synthesis）和验证（Validation）。将57个职业按交付物类型分为30个先验丰富的常规角色和27个先验稀疏的专业角色。FinProBench包含1,723个整理后的交付物，覆盖57个职业、8个金融子行业和161种交付物类型。

Result: 在常规角色中，Prompt-only与RGRC表现接近（89.2% vs 90.7%），但在角色专业化任务中RGRC显著优于Prompt-only（99.1% vs 78.0%）。人工交付物平均得分最高（73.7分），且角色级规则复用使每任务构建成本降低6.7倍。

Conclusion: 提示工程在常规角色中可近似达到规则质量，但超出模型先验知识的专业标准必须依赖专业实践的接地（grounding）。角色级规则复用显著降低构建成本，为金融AI智能体评估提供了更可靠的范式。

Abstract: Evaluating financial AI agents requires criteria aligned with real professional work. Existing rubric methods typically derive criteria from task prompts or model outputs, overlooking tacit standards visible only in practitioner deliverables. We introduce FinProBench, a benchmark for professional financial tasks, and Role-Grounded Rubric Construction (RGRC), a reusable pipeline that derives rubrics from deliverables produced by practitioners in the same role. RGRC comprises four stages: Deliverable Collection, Competency Extraction, Rubric Synthesis, and Validation. Its rubrics capture tacit standards, distinguish quality levels, and transfer across tasks within a role. Before analysis, we classified 57 occupations by deliverable genre into 30 prior-rich conventional roles and 27 prior-sparse role-specialized roles. Across all roles, Prompt-only nearly matches RGRC for conventional roles (89.2% vs. 90.7%), but RGRC substantially outperforms it for role-specialized roles (99.1% vs. 78.0%). This split indicates that prompt engineering can approximate rubrics when conventions are well represented in model priors, while professional grounding is essential for standards beyond those priors. FinProBench is built from 1,723 curated deliverables spanning 57 occupations, 8 financial sub-industries, and 161 deliverable types, and releases an initial evaluation set of 20 complete tasks covering 20 roles in 7 sub-industries. With heterogeneous LLM judges and role-level rubrics, human deliverables rank first on average (73.7 vs. 70.3, 70.2, and 69.6 out of 100), while all four systems show overlapping 95% confidence intervals and complementary strengths. Reusing rubrics at the role level reduces estimated per-task construction effort by 6.7 times relative to authoring each rubric from scratch.

</details>


### [233] [FinPerMA: A Theory-Informed, Event-Grounded Personalized-Memory Benchmark for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04095)
*Ben Wang,Kang Zhou,Lifan Guo,Feng Chen,Chi Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出FinPerMA，一个基于事件驱动场景的个性化记忆基准测试，用于评估LLM智能体在长期金融咨询中维护和更新用户模型的能力。在276个用户画像和2,994个问题上，即使是最先进的大语言模型和多种记忆配置也远未饱和，全上下文配置的准确率仅约47%，多选题准确率约39%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有个性化记忆基准主要测试事实记忆保留，或依赖约束较弱的模型生成轨迹，缺乏对事件驱动偏好适应的评估。在金融咨询等高风险的长期个性化场景中，LLM智能体能否跨长时间范围维护和更新个性化用户模型仍不明确。

Method: FinPerMA采用事件驱动的生成管道，结合确定性、基于理论的影响规则、受控LLM叙述和自动化质量筛选来构建基准。通过Post-Shock检查点来隔离智能体是否已将重大事件整合进其持久用户模型。评估了7个前沿LLM和最多7种记忆配置。

Result: 在2,994个问题、276个用户画像的评估中，所有模型和配置均远未达到饱和：全上下文配置总体准确率不超过约47%，多选题准确率不超过约39%。归因分析显示，基于摘要的记忆往往保留事实细节但丢失个性化所需的偏好信号，简单的检索方法可超越专门构建的记忆系统，且差距在冲击后进一步扩大。

Conclusion: 现有LLM智能体的个性化记忆能力在事件驱动的偏好适应方面仍存在显著不足，简单的检索方法可能比复杂记忆系统更有效，这为未来个性化记忆系统的设计提供了重要启示。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents are increasingly used as personalized assistants in high-stakes domains such as financial advising, yet it remains unclear whether they can maintain and update an individualized user model over long horizons. Existing personalized-memory benchmarks primarily test factual retention or rely on weakly constrained model-generated trajectories, leaving event-driven preference adaptation underexplored. We introduce FinPerMA, an event-grounded benchmark that evaluates personalized memory against frozen longitudinal investor trajectories. Its generation pipeline combines deterministic, theory-informed impact rules, controlled LLM narration, and automated quality screening; a Post-Shock checkpoint isolates whether an agent has integrated a material event into its persistent user model. On 2,994 questions from 276 personas, seven frontier LLMs and up to seven memory configurations remain far from saturated: no full-context configuration exceeds approximately 0.47 overall accuracy or approximately 39% on multiple-choice questions. Attribution analysis shows that summary-based memory often preserves factual details while losing the preference signals needed for personalization; simple retrieval can therefore outperform purpose-built memory systems, with the gap widening after shocks.

</details>


### [234] [BrainBench: Benchmarking Large Language Models for Comprehensive EEG Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04156)
*Yangxuan Zhou,Sha Zhao,Yuning Chen,Chen Wu,Jiquan Wang,Shijian Li,Gang Pan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个统一基准测试，用于评估大语言模型在"全面脑电图理解"能力上的表现，涵盖17个数据集、多个任务类别和超过10万次执行，从数值、类别、集合、序列、语义和伪影等多个维度进行验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有EEG分析评估主要聚焦于孤立的解码任务或系统特定演示，缺乏对LLM在"全面EEG理解"（即将自然语言指令、信号处理、定量证据和科学解释整合在一起的工作流）能力上的系统性量化评估。

Method: 构建统一基准测试（Benchmarkname），包含四个子集：基础分析、睡眠评估、神经认知评估和生理整合，覆盖17个数据集和多类任务。采用两种执行范式：CodeAct自主代码执行和BrainAgent结构化智能体分析，并通过数值、类别、集合、序列、语义和伪影六种验证方式对输出进行评估。

Result: 评估了多个代表性LLM在超过10万次执行中的表现，结果显示不同模型、子集、难度水平和执行范式之间的结果差异显著，表明EEG能力取决于模型本身及其操作化方式。

Conclusion: 该基准测试为推进基于LLM的EEG理解研究提供了可复现的测试平台，代码和基准将公开发布，评估结果将持续更新。

Abstract: Electroencephalography (EEG) analysis extends beyond assigning predefined labels to recordings; it requires workflows connecting natural-language instructions, signal processing, quantitative evidence, and scientific interpretation. We term this capability \emph{comprehensive EEG understanding}. Existing evaluations, however, primarily target isolated decoding tasks or system-specific demonstrations, leaving the competence of large language models (LLMs) insufficiently quantified. We introduce \benchmarkname{}, a unified benchmark for comprehensive, instruction-conditioned EEG understanding. It comprises four subsets---Foundational Analysis, Sleep Assessment, Neurocognitive Assessment, and Physiological Integration---covering 17 datasets, \numcases{} tasks, and over \numinstances{} real-data instances. Given an instruction and EEG recordings with optional physiological signals, a system must perform the analysis and produce a scientifically grounded report and, when required, artifacts. Outputs are assessed through numerical, categorical, set, sequence, semantic, and artifact validation. We evaluate \nummodels{} representative LLMs across more than 100K executions under two paradigms: autonomous code execution with CodeAct and structured agentic analysis with BrainAgent. Results vary substantially across models, subsets, difficulty levels, and execution paradigms, showing that EEG competence depends on the model and its operationalization. \benchmarkname{} provides a reproducible testbed for advancing LLM-based EEG understanding. The code and benchmark will be released soon, with evaluation results continuously updated.

</details>


### [235] [Adversarially Robust Abductive Fusion of Pre-trained Transformer-based Perception Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04190)
*Mario Leiva,Yue Ma,Qinru Qiu,Gerardo Simari,Paulo Shakarian*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需领域知识的元认知层（LVP），利用向量空间几何从每个模型的训练嵌入构建标签向量池，学习错误检测规则，在分布偏移和协同攻击下显著优于多数投票等组合方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练感知模型在新环境中因分布偏移导致精度下降，简单的模型组合（如多数投票）无法恢复精度，且对协同失败脆弱。现有元认知方法依赖手工设计的领域知识线索，无法迁移到真正新颖的场景。

Method: 利用向量空间几何构建每个模型独立的标签向量池（LVP），基于检测结果相对训练原型的几何关系生成错误检测规则；将多个ViT检测器的融合建模为基于一致性的溯因问题，在测试时通过精确整数规划（IP）和多项式时间启发式求解。

Result: 在15个天气偏移测试集和6个ViT检测器的航空影像基准上，无领域知识的方法在干净数据上与最强多数投票变体匹配（F1差在0.005以内）；在90%标签翻转攻击下平均F1为0.42，相对MV-Plurality的0.35提升22%，翻转率超过0.4时在所有测试集上取得最高F1。

Conclusion: 元认知错误检测层可以不依赖任何领域知识学习，通过向量空间几何达到与领域知识规则相当的性能；该方法保持神经符号框架，可与领域知识互补，且在协同攻击下展现出鲁棒性优势。

Abstract: Deploying pre-trained perception models in novel environments degrades their accuracy under distributional shift, and assembling them alone does not recover it: combiners such as majority voting trade recall for precision and are brittle to coordinated failures. Prior metacognitive methods learn logical rules that flag a model's errors, but rely on hand-authored domain-knowledge cues (object-size priors, segmentation masks) that do not transfer to genuinely novel scenes. We show that this metacognitive layer can be learned without any domain knowledge by exploiting vector-space geometry: per-model Label Vector Pools (LVP), built from each model's own training embeddings, yield error-detection rules from the geometry of detections relative to training-determined prototypes, reaching parity with domain-knowledge rules to within $0.002$ every F1 on test set. Because the approach remains neurosymbolic, these geometric rules share a single logical framework and can still be complemented by domain knowledge when available. We frame the fusion of multiple imperfect ViT-based detectors as a consistency-based abduction problem solved at test time by an exact Integer Program (IP) and a polynomial-time heuristic. On an aerial-imagery benchmark of 15 weather-shifted test sets and six ViT detectors, our domain-knowledge-free layer matches the strongest majority-vote variant on clean data (within $0.005$ F1) and, unlike every majority-vote baseline, retains its performance under a coordinated label-flipping attack: at a $90\%$ flip rate it averages $0.42$ F1 versus $0.35$ for MV-Plurality (a $22\%$ relative gain) and attains the highest F1 on \emph{every} test set once the flip rate exceeds $0.4$

</details>


### [236] [MatrAIx: Simulating the World with 8.3 Billion Persona Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04205)
*Xiaomin Li,Yuexing Hao,Jianheng Hou,Jintao Huang,Qianfeng Wen,Shirley Huang,Yifan Liu,Xiaoyi Liu,Yilan Fan,Yijun Wang,Koutian Wu,Ruoqi Gao,Muhammad Ahmed Mohsin,Jing Tang,Brihi Joshi,Heming Liu,Zheyuan Deng,Zonglin Di,Sankalp Jajee,Jiuyao Lu,Zhiwei Zhang,Saksham Kapoor,Ishan Gupta,Yunhan Zhao,Chanwoo Park,Yucheng Lu,Bing Hu,Weihang Xiao,Aravind Mohan,Hanwen Xing,Runyu Zhang,Mihir Kulshreshtha,Yuanda Xu,Qianyu Zhu,Dianzhuo Wang,Yuxin Xiao,Bowen Jiang,Yongye Su,Wenhao Chai,Zuxin Liu,Lawrence Yunliang Chen,Xuandong Zhao,Ethan Ye,Shivam Patel,Jason Xie,Alex Martin Richmond,Weixiang Ding,Emre Okcular,Diya Mathew,Ziheng Wang,Rana M. Shahroz Khan,Zhejian Peng,Fang Wu,Fan Nie,Xinyang Han,Yubin Kim,Jiawei Zhang,Zhenting Qi,Huangyuan Su,Xu Pan,Abinitha Gourabathina,Hyewon Jeong,Hemanth Neelgund Ramesh,Kumail Alhamoud,Kimia Hamidieh,Zidi Xiong,Samuel Schmidgall,Pengrui Han,Yepeng Huang,Yongheng Wang,Bowen Yang,Alex Gu,Yuchu Wang,Akshay Paruchuri,Brenna Li,Hejie Cui,Jiayuan Ding,Chaosheng Dong,Jiahao Wang,Yixuan He,Chi Wang,Pamela Bhattacharya,Tianyi Peng,Paul Pu Liang,Mitchell Gordon,Yilun Du,Marinka Zitnik,James Zou,Prasanna Tambe,Philip Torr,Emily Fox,Asu Ozdaglar,Dawn Song*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MatrAIx是一个大规模模拟用户评估基础设施，包含83亿个人物记录（Persona 8B）、四种评估环境（Survey、AI聊天机器人、Web和App）、1010个应用任务，以及18,189次评估试验，通过异质用户模拟来评估AI系统和数字产品，验证试验显示行为遵循率达91.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类评估AI系统和数字产品成本高昂、速度慢且难以规模化，现有的离线评估虽然可扩展但往往忽略了人类多样性和交互行为。

Method: MatrAIx包含三大核心组件：（1）Persona 8B包含83亿条人物记录、1290个分类维度，通过依赖图采样保持属性相关性，发布约100万条质量过滤的核心数据集（约60万人写和40万合成记录）；（2）MatrAIx Playground提供Survey、AI Chatbot、Web、App四种用户评估环境；（3）提供覆盖25+领域（商业、软件、金融、医疗等）的1010个应用任务。使用三种LLM（Claude Opus 4.8、GPT 5.5、Claude Haiku 4.5）驱动人物代理，在18,189次评估试验中收集反馈。

Result: 两个主要验证研究：（1）400次受控试验评估跨10个行为属性和4种环境的人物遵循度，366次（91.5%）正确表达或抑制了声明行为；（2）人类和LLM评委评估了人写人物的抽取质量。反馈捕获了价格涨幅后的犹豫、AI助手失败后的继续意愿、延迟容忍度等随人物背景变化的决策和偏好差异。

Conclusion: MatrAIx提供了一个端到端的评估基础设施，能够以异质模拟用户群体大规模评估AI系统和数字产品，兼顾扩展性与用户多样性。

Abstract: Human evaluation of AI systems and digital products is costly, slow, and difficult to scale. Offline evaluations are more scalable but often abstract away human diversity and interactive behavior. We therefore introduce MatrAIx, a population-scale simulated-user evaluation infrastructure for testing AI systems and digital products with heterogeneous users. MatrAIx has three core components: First, Persona 8B contains 8.3 billion persona records represented by 1,290 categorical dimensions. Records are either sampled from a dependency graph that preserves correlated attributes or derived from human-authored profiles. We release a quality-filtered coreset of approximately 1 million personas, comprising 599,847 human-grounded and 400,000 synthetic records. Second, the MatrAIx Playground provides four environments in which diverse users evaluate and interact with digital products: Survey, AI Chatbot, Web, and App. Third, MatrAIx provides 1,010 application tasks spanning more than 25 domains, including Commerce, Software, Finance, and Healthcare. We conducted 18,189 evaluation trials across eight representative tasks. Persona agents were powered by three LLMs: Claude Opus 4.8, GPT 5.5, and Claude Haiku 4.5. The resulting feedback captures how decisions and preferences vary across persona backgrounds, including hesitation after a price increase, willingness to continue after an AI assistant fails, and latency tolerance. We conducted two main validation studies: First, a 400-trial controlled study evaluated persona adherence across ten behavioral attributes and all four environments. The declared behavior was expressed or correctly suppressed in 366 trials (91.5%). Second, human and LLM judges evaluated the extraction quality of human-grounded personas. Overall, MatrAIx provides an end-to-end infrastructure for evaluating AI systems and digital products with diverse simulated human users.

</details>


### [237] [Interoceptive Attention as Dynamic Homeostatic Prioritization in a Foraging Agent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04232)
*St John Grimbly,Nicolas Kuske,Evert A. Boonstra,Bruce A. Bassett,Charel van Hoof,Rowan Hodson,Benjamin Rosman,Ryan Smith,Mark Solms,Jonathan P. Shock*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究有限感知带宽下的生物系统如何分配感知精度。在一个基于主动推理的四通道觅食网格世界（AffectWorld）中，代理每步识别最紧急的体内需求通道，并将固定的内感受精度预算重新分配给它。结果显示，这种选择性分配使学习阶段存活率翻倍以上（0.414 vs 0.199），且效益同时来自感知与规划两个层面，并与需求对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物系统必须在有限的感知带宽下调节相互竞争的需求，精化某一估计会牺牲其他估计的精度。因此，任何固定预算的系统都必须决定如何分配感知精度。作者希望通过主动推理框架研究这一精度分配问题。

Method: 构建基于主动推理的觅食代理，其需维持多个身体需求以满足生存。代理每步读取自身身体状态信念，识别最需要的通道，并将固定的内感受精度预算重新分配至该通道，使同一精度塑形的似然同时用于信念更新与规划。在AffectWorld四通道觅食网格世界中进行实验。

Result: 选择性精度分配在匹配预算下使学习阶段存活率翻倍以上（0.414 vs 0.199，跨11种布局，n=32种子）。若仅对规划者拒绝精度塑形似然，则效益损失约一半，说明效益同时通过感知与规划产生。将精度指向最不需要的通道比均匀分配更差。被关注的通道自身动力学学习速度约快两倍，即使在观测次数匹配时仍保持优势。

Conclusion: 选择性精度分配显著提升存活率，其机制既作用于感知也作用于规划，且与需求对齐。被关注通道的学习加速是精度路由的行为痕迹，体现在学习速度而非存活率上。

Abstract: Biological systems must regulate competing needs under limited perceptual bandwidth, where sharpening one estimate costs the capacity to sharpen the others. Any fixed-budget system therefore has to decide where to allocate its perceptual precision. We study this in a foraging agent that must keep several bodily needs satisfied to survive, modelled with active inference. At each step it reads its own body-state beliefs, identifies the most-needed channel, and reallocates a fixed budget of interoceptive precision toward it, so that the same precision-shaped likelihood feeds both belief update and planning. In AffectWorld, a four-channel foraging gridworld, this selective allocation more than doubles learning-phase survival at matched budget against a uniform-precision agent ($0.414$ vs $0.199$ across 11 layouts, $n{=}32$ seeds each, paired cluster-bootstrap $p \leq 10^{-4}$). Two further results sharpen the mechanism. The benefit runs through planning as well as perception, since denying the shaped likelihood to the planner alone removes about half of it. It is also need-aligned, since aiming precision at the least-needed channel does worse than spreading it evenly. The attended channel additionally learns its own dynamics about twice as fast, and stays ahead even at matched observation count, a behavioural trace of the same precision routing, visible in learning speed, not survival.

</details>


### [238] [CoPlan: A Trustworthy Co-Intelligence Interface for Care Planning through Role-Based Contestable Argument Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05107)
*Hung Truong Thanh Nguyen,Hélène Fournier,Piper Jackson,Makoto Itoh,Shannon Freeman,Rene Richard,Hung Cao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CoPlan——一个面向人类与AI协同护理规划的"共智能可争辩"界面。系统采用多智能体工作流，由专业AI智能体生成候选干预方案及支持或质疑性论点，人类护理规划者可在最终方案生成前接受、拒绝、修改或补充论点，从而结合了人类与AI的互补专长，同时保持推荐方案的可检查、可修订和可论证性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI辅助护理规划虽有助于协调复杂的临床、功能、心理社会和环境需求，但多数AI系统将推荐作为固定输出呈现，限制了利益相关者检查、挑战和修订方案的能力，尤其当AI建议与临床判断、患者价值观或现实可行性冲突时，这一问题尤为突出。

Method: CoPlan采用多智能体工作流：专门AI智能体生成候选干预措施及支持性或挑战性论点，人类护理规划者可在最终方案生成前接受、拒绝、修改或添加论点。系统具备自适应护理团队招募、基于角色的论点审查、最终护理方案生成及通过调度智能体实现的实际跟进功能。

Result: 在"居家养老"（aging-in-place）护理规划场景中展示了CoPlan的有效性，证明了该设计能够支持自适应护理团队招募、角色化论点审查、最终方案生成和调度跟进等功能。

Conclusion: 本研究贡献了一个可争辩的护理规划界面，以及一个可信赖人机协同护理规划的设计框架，该框架在保留人类主体性和临床问责制的同时，实现人类与AI的协作决策。

Abstract: AI-supported care planning can help clinicians, patients, caregivers, and care teams coordinate complex decisions across clinical, functional, psychosocial, and environmental needs. However, many AI systems present recommendations as fixed outputs, limiting stakeholders' ability to inspect, challenge, and revise plans when they conflict with clinical judgment, patient values, or real-world feasibility. We present CoPlan - a Co-Intelligent and Contestable Interface for Human-AI Care Planning. CoPlan uses a multi-agent workflow in which specialized AI agents generate candidate interventions and supporting or challenging arguments, while human care planners can accept, reject, modify, or add arguments before final plan generation. Through this design, CoPlan combines co-intelligence, in which humans and AI agents contribute complementary expertise, with contestability, where recommendations remain open to inspection, revision, and justification. We demonstrate CoPlan in an aging-in-place care planning scenario. The system supports adaptive care team recruitment, role-based argument review, final care plan generation, and practical follow-up through scheduling agents. This work contributes a contestable care planning interface and a design framing for trustworthy human-AI care planning that preserves human agency and clinical accountability.

</details>


### [239] [The RAIL Principles for Neurosymbolic AI: Reasoning, Assurances, Interfacing and Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04285)
*Agnese Chiatti,Michael Cochez,Cristina Cornelio,Sebastijan Dumancic,Artur d'Avila Garcez,Luis C. Lamb,Lia Morra,Mathias Niepert,Robert Peharz,Alberto Speranzon,Maarten Stol,Annette Ten Teije,Thiviyan Thanapalasingam,Frank Van Harmelen,Emile Van Krieken,Antonio Vergari,Benjie Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了神经符号AI设计的四大原则（RAIL：推理、保证、接口、学习），并论证神经符号AI并非小众方法，而是众多已成功技术的关键所在。通过RAIL框架统一分析各类AI系统（包括物理感知机器学习、Alpha系列、因果学习及工具增强的大语言模型），为工程师设计生产级AI系统提供更明智的决策指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经符号AI系统（结合机器学习与符号推理）正受到越来越多的关注，但常被视为一种小众方法。作者认为这种组合对开发可靠、高效且值得信赖的AI系统至关重要，需要重新审视当前AI系统的设计方式，并为此提供一个统一的分析框架。

Method: 提出RAIL（Reasoning, Assurances, Interfacing, Learning）四大原则作为神经符号AI设计的分析框架，并运用该框架对各类主流AI系统（从物理感知机器学习到神经引导搜索，再到因果学习和工具增强的大语言模型）进行统一视角的分析与重构。

Result: 展示了RAIL框架能够为看似各不相关的AI系统提供统一的分析视角，揭示了众多领先AI系统（包括传统上不被视为神经符号的系统）均可通过这四条原则进行解析，并使工程师能够在生产级AI系统的设计和部署中做出更明智、更原则化的决策。

Conclusion: RAIL原则为神经符号AI提供了系统化的设计指南，能够指导实践者将神经符号方法整合到下一代AI技术中，从而推动更可靠、高效和值得信赖的AI系统的发展。

Abstract: Neurosymbolic AI systems that integrate machine learning and symbolic reasoning are rapidly gaining attention. They complement the data-intensive statistical approaches of neural networks and language models with symbolic reasoning algorithms to function in high-stakes domains or in low-data regimes that characterize many real-world applications.
