This paper presents a domain-aware retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) pipeline for embedded C software reuse detection that addresses the hardware-compatibility gap directly. The pipeline enriches each function by extracting its existing inline comments, call-graph context, and a project README before embedding it with eight backbone models (MiniLM, MPNet, BGE, E5, GraphCodeBERT, OpenAI text-embedding-3-small, LLaMA 3 8B, StarCoder2 3B) acting as feature extractors. Four hardware-compatibility validators---covering peripheral token overlap, parameter count parity, call-graph dependency overlap, and structural branching pattern---filter candidates directly in the retrieval stack. Evaluated on six public embedded C software projects (184 functions, 4,815 above-plateau pairs), the pipeline reveals that SonarQube produces a 93.6% false-positive rate as a reuse filter, with 83.5% of failures caused by hardware-environment mismatches that static analysis cannot detect. Manual verification of 40 rejected pairs confirms 97.5% validator accuracy, and a diagnostic rule-injection variant identifies the dominant failure categories (McNemar chi-squared~=~294.0, p~$<$~0.001).

</details>


### [273] [AgentForge: An Immersive Role-Playing Platform for Learning Agentic Software Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04148)
*Zihan Fang,Yueke Zhang,Yu Huang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出AgentForge，一个沉浸式学习系统，让新手在多智能体代码修复工作流中扮演四种软件工程角色之一（任务规划者、补丁编写者、代码审查者、测试执行者），通过角色脚手架和元认知支持帮助新手理解智能体AI协作并提升软件工程技能。37名新手参与的实验表明任务完成率高，但不同角色交互需求差异显著，参与者报告了对软件修复和智能体协作理解的显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体AI在软件开发中越来越普遍，但其决策和交互过程缺乏透明度，且现有系统假设用户能有效引导AI决策并验证其输出。这一假设对新手尤为困难，因为他们需要同时学习智能体AI的工作原理、有效协作方式和批判性评估输出的能力。

Method: AgentForge是一个沉浸式学习系统，新手在四角色（任务规划者、补丁编写者、代码审查者、测试执行者）中选择其一执行任务，其余三个角色由AI代理完成。系统通过基于角色的脚手架和元认知支持，使代理协调和中间产物可见，并鼓励新手监控和评估自身决策。研究招募了37名新手开发者进行实验评估。

Result: 实验结果显示参与者在新手与AI代理支持下实现了高任务完成率，但不同实践场景的交互需求差异显著：代码审查者实践需要更多的交互轮次、重路由和更长的完成时间（调整后p值=0.004），且被认为最具挑战性。参与者报告了对软件修复和代理协作理解的显著提升（调整后p值<0.001）。

Conclusion: AgentForge能够帮助新手在实践软件工程技能的同时，学会更批判性和有效地与智能体AI协作，为培养面向智能体AI时代的软件开发教育提供了有效的学习工具。

Abstract: Agentic AI is increasingly used to coordinate planning, implementation, review, and testing in software development, yet it often offers limited transparency into its decisions and interactions. Many such systems also assume that users can effectively guide the AI's decisions and validate its outputs. This assumption poses a particular challenge for novices, who must simultaneously learn how agentic AI works, how to collaborate with it effectively, and how to evaluate its outputs critically. To address this challenge, we present \textit{AgentForge}, an immersive learning system in which novices take on one of four software-engineering roles: Task Planner, Patch Author, Code Reviewer, or Test Runner, within a multi-agent code-repair workflow. In each practice session, the novices perform their chosen role while AI agents perform the remaining three. Through role-based scaffolding and metacognitive support, AgentForge clarifies role-specific responsibilities, makes agent coordination and intermediate artifacts visible, and encourages novices to monitor and evaluate their decisions. In a study with 37 novice developers, participants achieved high task-completion rates with AI-agent support. However, interaction demands differed significantly across practices: the Code Reviewer practice required more interaction turns, reroutes, and completion time ($p_{\mathrm{adj}} = .004$) and was perceived as the most challenging. Participants nevertheless reported significant gains in their understanding of software repair and agent collaboration ($p_{\mathrm{adj}} < .001$). These findings suggest that AgentForge can help novices develop practical software-engineering skills while learning to collaborate with agentic AI more critically and effectively.

</details>


### [274] [MergeSE: Post-Hoc Model Merging for Software Engineering Tasks Without Retraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04181)
*Palash R. Roy,Banani Roy,Kevin A. Schneider,Chanchal K. Roy*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: MergeSE是一个开源命令行和网页工具，支持对HuggingFace编码器检查点进行免训练模型合并，解决代码模型在不同分布下的碎片化问题。它提供五种操作（tasks、inspect、merge、evaluate、export），支持五种合并算法，在CPU上5秒内即可完成两个124M参数检查点的合并。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微调后的代码模型往往成为领域专家，在分布偏移下性能严重退化（如跨语言克隆检测F1下降71%）。虽然模型合并可以解决这一问题并达到多任务训练93%的性能，但缺乏实用的工具让软件工程研究者诊断检查点兼容性、合并专家模型并验证部署。

Method: MergeSE提供了五个核心操作：tasks（任务注册表）、inspect（检查点兼容性诊断）、merge（模型合并）、evaluate（基准验证）和export（导出部署）。支持TIES、DARE-TIES、Wudi、PCB和平均五种合并算法，能检测跨任务分类头不匹配，产生可种子化的确定性输出，并内置基准样本用于冒烟测试复现。

Result: 两个124M参数检查点的完整合并在CPU上5秒内完成。端到端验证表明MergeSE生成的检查点与参考实现匹配，并能从领域特定专家模型中恢复跨域性能。

Conclusion: MergeSE填补了软件工程领域中免训练模型合并工具的空缺，支持九种任务类型，涵盖漏洞检测、缺陷预测和代码异味检测等工作流，为SE研究者提供了从诊断到部署的完整工具链。

Abstract: Fine-tuned code models often behave as domain specialists and can degrade sharply under distribution shift: in our clone-detection setting, a model trained on same-language clones drops 71\% F1 on cross-language clones, while multi-task training falls to 0.151 F1 on unseen AI-generated clones. Our companion study shows that post-hoc model merging can address this fragmentation, achieving 93\% of multi-task performance without training data while generalizing 4$\times$ better to unseen clone types. However, no practical tool exists that lets SE researchers diagnose checkpoint compatibility, merge specialists, validate results on SE benchmarks, and export models for deployment. We present \textbf{MergeSE}, an open-source CLI and web tool for training-free model merging of HuggingFace encoder checkpoints. While motivated by OOD generalization in clone detection, MergeSE supports SE classification workflows more broadly through a built-in registry of nine task types, including vulnerability detection, defect prediction, and code-smell detection. MergeSE provides five operations: \textit{tasks}, \textit{inspect}, \textit{merge}, \textit{evaluate}, and \textit{export}. It supports five merging algorithms, including TIES, DARE-TIES, Wudi, PCB, and averaging; detects cross-task classification-head mismatches; produces seedable deterministic outputs; and includes bundled benchmark samples for smoke-test reproduction. A full merge of two 124M-parameter checkpoints completes in under 5 seconds on CPU. End-to-end validation confirms that MergeSE-produced checkpoints match reference implementations and recover cross-domain performance from domain-specific specialists. The tool is available online at https://mergese.usask.ca, and the development repository is at https://github.com/srlabUsask/MergeSE.

</details>


### [275] [SONAR: Task-Aware Code Summary Evaluation for LLM Consumers Without References](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04195)
*Simantika Bhattacharjee Dristi,Matthew B. Dwyer*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出SONAR框架，一种基于代码再生的参考无关评测方法，从正确性、抽象性、简洁性和流畅性四个维度评估源代码摘要，发现正确性和抽象性与下游LLM任务性能高度相关，而简洁性和流畅性对LLM消费者影响不大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统代码摘要评测从人类开发者视角出发，但LLM工具和智能体越来越多地消费代码摘要用于软件工程任务，现有评测标准忽视了这一现实，缺乏从消费者（LLM）角度评估摘要质量的框架。

Method: 提出SONAR参考无关评测框架，包含四个质量维度（正确性、抽象性、简洁性、流畅性）；核心创新是代码再生方法——利用摘要重新生成代码，以重建质量作为摘要质量的信号，无需参考摘要或人类/LLM主观判断。

Result: 在四个下游SE任务中，正确性（其次是抽象性）与LLM性能显著相关，相关性比最佳基线高14倍；简洁性和流畅性对LLM消费者影响不显著；对11个流行LLM的大规模评估揭示了各模型在不同质量维度上的优劣。

Conclusion: 摘要的实用性取决于任务和消费者类型；人类开发者重视的简洁性和流畅性对LLM消费者并不重要，需要任务感知的摘要生成方法。

Abstract: Source code summaries have traditionally been evaluated from a human developer's perspective, with quality determined by how closely they resemble developer-written references and how well they align with human preferences. But this overlooks a growing reality: LLM-based tools and agents increasingly consume code summaries as inputs for software engineering (SE) tasks, and what makes a summary useful for a consuming agent on a task remains largely unexplored. To bridge this gap, we propose SONAR, a reference-free framework that evaluates source code summaries along four dimensions: Correctness, Abstraction, Conciseness, and Fluency. Rather than optimizing for a pre-written "gold standard", SONAR introduces a novel code regeneration-based approach that uses a summary to regenerate code and leverages that reconstruction as a quality signal of the summary. This provides an empirical grounding that requires neither a reference summary nor the subjective judgment of humans or LLMs. We evaluate SONAR's dimensions on their ability to influence LLM performance across four downstream SE tasks. We find that Correctness, followed by Abstraction, significantly correlates with LLM performance, with correlations up to 14X higher than the best baseline. Conciseness and Fluency, though widely valued by human developers, remain mostly insignificant to an LLM consumer, suggesting that what makes a summary useful is task- and consumer-dependent. Through a large-scale evaluation of 11 popular LLMs using SONAR, we further identify the strengths and weaknesses of different models across each quality dimension, while offering insights to facilitate future research on task-aware summarization.

</details>


### [276] [A Unified Model for Cross-Domain Clone Detection via Model Merging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04215)
*Palash R. Roy,Banani Roy,Kevin A. Schneider,Chanchal K. Roy*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了模型合并（model merging）技术用于构建跨域代码克隆检测器。通过评估五种任务向量参数合并方法、架构拼接和跨tokenizer对齐方法，发现基于同一预训练基座的TIES合并能有效创建跨域检测器，在UniXcoder上达到0.865的合并F1分数（达到多任务训练的93%），且对未见AI生成克隆的泛化能力优于多任务训练达4倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代码克隆类型日益多样化（从语法副本到跨语言语义克隆再到AI生成重复代码），导致克隆检测领域的碎片化危机。现有深度学习检测器是领域专家，在训练分布外性能严重退化（F1下降超70%），而部署多个专用模型不切实际，同时训练单一跨域检测器又需要同时访问所有训练数据。

Method: 作者研究了三类模型合并技术：(1) 五种任务向量方法的参数合并（parameter merging）；(2) 通过贪心层拼接的架构合并（architecture merging）；(3) 跨tokenizer对齐。在四个代码模型、三个基准和十二种配置上进行系统评估。

Result: 同基座TIES合并能有效创建跨域检测器，在两个模型家族和三个随机种子上验证，UniXcoder上合并F1达0.865，为多任务性能的93%，且合并阶段无需任何训练数据。WUDI达到最高域内合并F1（0.899），但TIES对未见AI生成克隆泛化更好。跨基座合并在五种方法中仅产生边际且高方差的增益。合并检测器在GPTCloneBench上优于零样本代码LLM且推理成本更低，对未见AI生成克隆的泛化比多任务训练好达4倍。

Conclusion: 共享预训练基座的任务向量兼容性是有效合并的关键因素；合并检测器在域内性能与OOD鲁棒性之间存在权衡；TIES是推荐方法。这是模型合并用于软件工程的首批系统实证研究之一，为构建跨域克隆检测器提供了实用方案。

Abstract: The growing diversity of code clone types, from syntactic copies to cross-language semantic clones to AI-generated duplicates, has created a fragmentation crisis in clone detection. Current deep learning detectors are domain specialists that degrade significantly outside their training distribution, with F1 drops exceeding 70% across domains. Deploying multiple specialized models is impractical, yet training a single cross-domain detector requires simultaneous access to all training data. To address this, we investigate model merging, a family of post-hoc techniques that operate solely on trained checkpoints. We evaluate parameter merging with five task-vector methods, architecture merging via greedy layer stitching, and cross-tokenizer alignment across four code models, three benchmarks, and twelve configurations. Same-base TIES merging creates effective cross-domain detectors, validated across two model families and three random seeds, reaching 0.865 combined F1 on UniXcoder, 93% of multi-task performance without any training data at the merging step. WUDI achieves the highest in-distribution combined F1 at 0.899, but TIES generalizes better to unseen AI-generated clones, making it our recommended method. Cross-base merging yields only marginal and high-variance gains across all five methods, indicating that task vector compatibility through a shared pre-trained base is the binding factor for effective merging. Merged detectors also outperform zero-shot code LLMs on GPTCloneBench at lower inference cost and generalize up to 4x better than multi-task training to unseen AI-generated clones, suggesting a trade-off between in-domain performance and OOD robustness. This work provides one of the first systematic empirical studies of model merging for software engineering and a practical recipe for building cross-domain clone detectors.

</details>


### [277] [Neuro-Symbolic Proof-of-Vulnerability Generation with Open-Weight Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04217)
*Yu Nong,Haipeng Cai*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: POVGEN是一个低成本的神经符号框架，通过语义聚焦和LLM引导的约束推理（使用开源权重模型）来自动生成Proof-of-Vulnerability（PoV），在基准测试中达到78.98%的成功率，并能在250个真实CVE中为74.80%的案例生成有效PoV，同时发现了多个已确认的安全漏洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件漏洞验证困难，公开的触发输入往往不可用。现有技术在有效性、可扩展性、成本和可控性上各有取舍，缺少一个低成本的补充方案来自动生成PoV。

Method: POVGEN采用三阶段流程：首先定位漏洞相关代码区域（可利用补丁信息），然后进行路径敏感的可达性分析，最后通过LLM引导的约束推理结合SMT求解器来提取和求解约束以生成PoV。使用开源权重模型实现本地运行，无每次API调用成本。

Result: 在基准测试中成功生成78.98%的PoV，优于模糊测试（最高50.20%）和符号执行（2.45%）。在250个无公开PoV的真实CVE中，74.80%生成了有效PoV，无补丁信息时仍能复现65.1%。生成的PoV发现了6个有缺陷的补丁和5个先前未报告的安全漏洞（其中4个已确认并修复）。

Conclusion: POVGEN证明了神经符号方法结合开源LLM可以在低成本下高效生成PoV，其效果优于传统模糊测试和符号执行，并且在实际漏洞披露中具有实用价值（发现缺陷补丁和新漏洞）。

Abstract: Software vulnerabilities are persistent, but validating them remains difficult: a Proof-of-Vulnerability (PoV) requires a concrete input that triggers the vulnerable behavior, yet public triggering inputs are often unavailable for disclosed vulnerabilities. Existing techniques make different tradeoffs in effectiveness, scalability, cost, and controllability, leaving room for complementary designs. To complement them, we present POVGEN, a low-cost neuro-symbolic framework that makes PoV generation cost-effective via semantic focusing and LLM-guided constraint reasoning using open-weight models. POVGEN first localizes vulnerability-relevant regions (utilizing patch information if available), then performs path-sensitive reachability analysis, and finally generates PoVs by extracting and solving constraints with LLM-guided reasoning backed by an SMT solver. POVGEN successfully generates PoVs for 78.98% of vulnerabilities in a recent benchmark, outperforming fuzzing (up to 50.20%) and symbolic execution (2.45%). On 250 real-world CVEs without public PoVs, it generates valid PoVs for 74.80% of cases and reproduces 65.1% when without patch information. The fine-tuned open-weight models match frontier commercial LLMs on key sub-tasks (i.e., the core constraint-reasoning steps) while running locally at no per-sample API cost. Applying the generated PoVs revealed six flawed patches in disclosed CVEs (all subsequently fixed) and five previously unreported vulnerabilities (of which four have been confirmed and fixed by the developers).

</details>


### [278] [CURATE: Leveraging LLM Agents to Compose, Catalog, and Deploy Reproducible Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04270)
*Nolan Cutler,Chia-Chen Kuo,Nanda Velugoti,Kathryn Newhart,Renato Figueiredo*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出CURATE系统，一个基于LLM的人机协同多智能体框架，用于管理可组合工作流的全生命周期（包括部署和共享），弥补现有编码智能体仅关注代码生成的不足。通过模块目录支持模块复用，并在6个实验中验证了可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有编码智能体主要聚焦于代码生成，未能覆盖工作流的全生命周期（如部署和共享）。用户因此需要自行拼接模块并管理部署，增加了使用门槛，尤其在应用科学领域阻碍了LLM加速开发的优势发挥。

Method: 提出CURATE框架（Composition, User-in-the-loop, Reuse, Automated Task Execution），采用人机协同的多智能体系统，利用LLM智能体管理工作流的全生命周期。核心特性是模块目录（catalog），支持跨工作流的模块存储与复用，并可通过扩展子图来支持FAIR原则（可查找、可访问、可互操作、可复用）。

Result: 基于Claude Opus 4.8构建了初始原型，进行了6个实验：复现和适配来自SeBS-Flow基准测试套件的4个工作流，以及自动化开发和扩展一个用于环境工程厌氧消化模拟的复杂机理模型工作流。

Conclusion: CURATE系统展示了通过模块目录支持工作流全生命周期管理的可行性，有望降低应用科学领域的工作流开发门槛，并为支持FAIR原则提供基础。

Abstract: Agentic code generation has shown promise in automating and accelerating software development by utilizing Large Language Models (LLMs) to generate, test, and deploy code. For engineers and scientists, such systems have the potential to accelerate the development of applied and scientific workflows while reducing barriers to entry in domains that have yet to fully realize their benefits. However, a key gap remains: existing coding agents primarily focus on code generation and do not address the entire workflow lifecycle, including deployment and sharing. As a result, users develop and stitch modules independently while managing deployment on their own. To address this gap, we propose CURATE - Composition, User-in-the-loop, Reuse, and Automated Task Execution - a novel human-in-the-loop multi-agent system that uses LLM agents to manage and develop composable workflows across their entire lifecycle. A key feature of the system is a catalog that allows for the storage and reuse of modules across workflows. Module catalogs provide a foundation that can be expanded to support FAIR principles by facilitating the sharing and reuse of curated modules and subgraphs. We demonstrate the feasibility of our system with an initial prototype using Claude Opus 4.8, comprising 6 experiments: reproducing and adapting 4 workflows derived from the SeBS-Flow benchmark suite, and automating the development and scaling of a workflow that leverages a complex mechanistic model in environmental engineering used to simulate anaerobic digestion.

</details>


### [279] [LLM-based Vulnerability Discovery in Business Process Documentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04271)
*Ben Falchuk,Himanshu Garg,Euthimios Panagos,Sioan Zohar*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文介绍MIRABELLE系统，利用AI/ML技术从业务流程文档中识别和表征业务逻辑漏洞，重点探讨了大型语言模型（LLMs）在该系统中各关键阶段的表现，从语法错误标记到完整流程结构恢复与提取。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 业务流程与软件和硬件一样存在漏洞，可能导致产品质量问题、延误和成本增加。然而从自然语言文档中提取业务逻辑面临领域专业知识要求高、流程复杂、信息量大的挑战，需要有效的自动化方法。

Method: MIRABELLE系统利用AI/ML技术处理业务流程文档（包括ISO文档、用户指南、工作说明和流程执行日志），生成带属性的图表示，结合图方法和形式逻辑方法识别潜在漏洞。本文重点实验评估多种LLM在漏洞检测各关键阶段的性能。

Result: 报告了多种LLM在漏洞检测关键阶段的表现，涵盖从短语句的语法和技术错误标记，到完整业务流程结构恢复与提取的性能评估。

Conclusion: MIRABELLE系统展示了从自然语言业务流程文档中自动识别漏洞的可行性，LLM在该流程中发挥重要作用，但其性能在不同任务阶段存在差异，仍需进一步优化。

Abstract: Just like software and hardware, business processes are susceptible to vulnerabilities that can lead to product quality issues, delays, and increased costs. Business process vulnerabilities can arise from a variety of sources, including conflicting requirements, ambiguous documentation, invalid measurement spec-ifications, omission of quality checks, or implementations that differ from speci-fications. MIRABELLE is a system that identifies and characterizes business logic (BL) vulnerabilities from available business process representations, in-cluding ISO 9000/9001 documentation, user guides, work instructions, and pro-cess execution logs. MIRABELLE leverages recent advances in AI/ML to pro-cess available business process documentation and generate attributed graph rep-resentations of the business logic that can be processed using both graph and for-mal logic approaches for identifying potential vulnerabilities. However, extract-ing the business logic (e.g., operation execution sequences, decisions, input/out-put resources) from mostly natural language artifacts is challenging due to the required domain expertise, inherent process complexity, and the sometimes very large volumes of information. This paper focuses on our experimentation with Large Language Models (LLMs) and their role within MIRABELLE. We report on the performance of several LLMs across vital stages of vulnerability detection, from grammatical and technical error-flagging in short phrasings, to complete process structure recovery and extraction.

</details>


### [280] [EA-Graph: Artifact-Anchored Verification Memory for Coding Agents under Upstream Drift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04278)
*Hwai-Jung Hsu,Cheng-Jan Chi,Hanna Everett*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: EA-Graph是一种基于工件锚定的验证声明记忆系统，在子路径粒度上表示代码工件并锚定验证声明到其依赖内容。在涵盖42个会话、7个生成仓库的评估中，artifact-anchored记忆在更小的模型上显著优于散文笔记和无记忆条件（所有配对Wilcoxon检验p=0.0156），且未出现虚构缺失内容的情况。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编码代理在多会话工作中，散文笔记只能保存结论而无法保留支持该结论的程序状态。当上游代码发生变化后，即使仓库仍然可以构建，之前的验证声明可能已不再有效，导致代理基于过时或无效的验证信息做出错误判断。

Method: EA-Graph以子路径粒度表示工件，将别名解析为叶级定义，将每个验证声明锚定到用于建立该声明的内容，并将证据强度与新鲜度分离。当替换内容不可用时，声明被标记为不可证明而非猜测。评估使用行为到工件ground truth已知的生成仓库，在值漂移、逻辑漂移和故意扣留上游内容三种条件下分类声明的状态。

Result: 在Haiku轮中，artifact-anchored记忆在所有7个世界中均优于散文笔记和无持久记忆；每个精确配对的Wilcoxon比较p=0.0156。在Sonnet轮中，锚定条件表现完美但对照组频繁达到上限导致预设对比不显著。所有会话中均未虚构扣留的内容。

Conclusion: 工件锚定记忆改善了较小模型在可证明性判断上的表现。探索性比较表明结构化的声明记忆可能通过外部化会话内重新推导来缩小能力差距，但并未建立跨模型等价性。该研究不对效率或修复质量作任何声明。

Abstract: Coding agents increasingly work across sessions, but prose notes can preserve a conclusion without the program state that supported it. After an upstream change, a repository may still build even though earlier verification claims are no longer valid. EA-Graph is an artifact-anchored memory for verification claims. It represents artifacts at sub-path granularity, resolves aliases to leaf definitions, anchors each claim to the content used to establish it, and keeps evidence strength separate from freshness. When replacement content is unavailable, the claim becomes unprovable rather than guessed.
  EA-Graph is evaluated on generated repositories whose behavior-to-artifact ground truth is known by construction. The task is to classify prior claims as unaffected, affected, or unprovable after value drift, logic drift, and deliberately withheld upstream content. The analysis covers 42 sessions across seven clean worlds, 14 model-world instances, three memory conditions, and two model tiers. In the Haiku round, artifact-anchored memory outscored prose notes and no persistent memory in all seven worlds; each exact paired Wilcoxon comparison yielded p = 0.0156. In the Sonnet round, the anchored condition was perfect, but frequent control ceilings left the preregistered contrasts non-significant. No session fabricated withheld content. These results support a bounded claim: artifact-anchored memory improved the smaller model's provability judgments in this testbed. An exploratory comparison further suggests that structured claim memory may narrow a capability gap by externalizing in-session re-derivation, but it does not establish cross- model equivalence. The study makes no claim about efficiency or repair quality.

</details>


### [281] [COMPAS: Difficulty-Aware Joint Search for Optimizing Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04336)
*Jingzhi Gong,Jie M. Zhang,Gunel Jahangirova,Dong Huang,Mohammad Reza Mousavi,Mark Harman*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Code generation systems make each LLM call with a model, a prompt, and decoding settings. However, existing optimization methods usually tune only part of these choices or use one fixed configuration for all tasks: global optimizers search one configuration for all tasks, routers choose only a model, and prompt optimizers keep the model and decoding settings fixed. This leaves their joint, group-specific interactions unclear. We therefore examine how these choices interact and observe that prompts and decoding settings interact, tuning effects vary by model, and the best configuration varies by task difficulty. Guided by these observations, we introduce COMPAS (Code-generation Optimization over Models, Prompts, And Decoding Settings), a difficulty-aware method that learns group-specific quality-cost fronts through low-cost model selection and joint prompt-decoding search, then routes each test task to its matching front online without further search. Under a matched search budget on LiveCodeBench, COMPAS improves pass@1 from 45.9% for the best baseline to 52.8% while reducing cost from $36.57 to $4.92. This also transfers to repository-level code generation on SWE-bench, resolving 76.0% of tasks versus 70.0% for the best baseline. Code and the reproducibility artifact are available at https://github.com/gjz78910/COMPAS.

</details>


### [282] [ExeCRE: Execution-Consistency Guided Reliability Estimation for Self-Correcting Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04439)
*Yiru Dong,Richong Zhang,Fanshuang Kong,Si Chen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出ExeCRE框架，通过统计大量随机输入下代码执行输出的一致性模式，使用Dawid-Skene模型推断代码可靠性，替代传统的测试或LLM反馈作为监督信号，从而减少自我纠正管线中的误导性反馈，显著提升代码生成的准确性和稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于代码执行反馈的自我纠正方法依赖可靠性未知的监督信号（如测试用例或LLM判断），容易引入误导性反馈，导致不必要的修订和错误的最终答案。

Method: ExeCRE通过三步实现：1）在大量随机生成的输入上收集候选代码的执行输出；2）将输出投影为一致性信号；3）应用Dawid-Skene统计模型推断潜在代码可靠性，并将其集成到自我纠正管线中用于代码生成。

Result: 在GPT-5.2和LiveCodeBench上，ExeCRE将已正确代码上误导性反馈的平均数量从基线方法的113.2降至14.0；同时一致地提升了有效性和稳定性，并在代码数学推理任务上观察到类似收益。

Conclusion: ExeCRE通过统计执行一致性分析提供了更可靠的代码可靠性估计，使生成代码在执行型管线中得到更可信的利用，为代码生成自我纠正提供了一种无需外部监督信号的替代方案。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have made notable progress in code generation, but they still struggle on challenging tasks that require sophisticated algorithms or complex implementations. Recent methods increasingly use code execution as feedback, especially in self-correction pipelines that construct verification signals from generated code. However, these pipelines often depend on supervision signals whose reliability is unknown, which can introduce misleading feedback, unnecessary revisions, and incorrect final answers. To address this issue, we propose ExeCRE, an Execution-Consistency guided code Reliability Estimation framework. Instead of judging candidate code by tests or LLM feedback, ExeCRE estimates code reliability by statistically analyzing consistency patterns in execution outputs over a large number of randomly generated inputs. It collects execution outputs over generated inputs, projects them into consistency signals, and applies the Dawid-Skene model to infer latent code reliability. We integrate ExeCRE into self-correction for code generation. Experiments show that ExeCRE consistently improves both effectiveness and stability, while substantially reducing misleading correction signals. Under GPT-5.2 on LiveCodeBench, the average number of misleading feedback cases on already correct code drops from 113.2 with a representative self-correction baseline to 14.0 with ExeCRE. As an additional study, we apply the same reliability estimation strategy to code-based mathematical reasoning and observe similar benefits. These results suggest that ExeCRE enables more reliable use of generated code in execution-based pipelines.

</details>


### [283] [AdaptAgent: A Multi-agent, Domain-Guided Reasoning Framework for Code Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04459)
*Xiaokai Rong,Hridya Dhulipala,Aashish Yadavally,Tien N. Nguyen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出AdaptAgent，一个基于多智能体和领域引导推理的代码适配框架，将LLM生成的代码或在线片段适配到目标仓库中。该框架通过五个专业智能体（意图摘要器、策略代理、领域规划器、上下文挖掘器和代码适配器）协作，生成最小化的统一diff补丁，在真实数据集上优于强基线，消融实验验证了各智能体的必要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开发者在将LLM生成代码或在线论坛代码片段集成到现有项目时面临巨大挑战，手动适配过程繁琐且容易出错，现有方法缺乏系统的代码适配框架。

Method: AdaptAgent采用多智能体协作架构，将代码适配任务分解为五个专门化智能体：Intent Summarizer从问答文本中提取适配目标，Policy Agent从六个适配类别中推导领域策略，Domain Planner生成自排序计划，Context Miner从目标代码库中提炼同级方法语义，Code Adapter基于编译器验证器迭代优化生成最小化统一diff。

Result: 在真实数据集上，AdaptAgent在语义正确性上优于强基线，生成的补丁能反映开发者的实际适配模式；消融实验表明每个智能体都不可或缺，其中规划器对代码加固和异常处理至关重要，意图摘要器对逻辑定制至关重要。

Conclusion: AdaptAgent通过智能体分工和领域引导推理，实现了将代码片段有效适配到现有项目的自动化方案，验证了多智能体架构在代码适配任务中的有效性。

Abstract: Developers often need to adapt into their projects the code generated from LLMs or code snippets from online forums. However, integrating them into an existing repository remains challenging in a manual process. A successful integration typically requires more than copying code as a user must produce correct adapting changes at a designated location in the target repository. We formalize this as the code adaptation problem: given a snippet, functional intent, a target repository, and an adaptation location, generate a patch that adapts the snippet into the repository. We present AdaptAgent, a multi-agent, domain-guided reasoning framework for code adaptation. Rather than relying on single-shot prompting, AdaptAgent decomposes adaptation into specialized agents that communicate via typed artifacts: an Intent Summarizer extracts adaptation goals from Q&A text; a Policy Agent derives domain policies from six adaptation categories; a Domain Planner generates a self-ordered plan; a Context Miner distills sibling-method semantics from the target codebase; and a Code Adapter realizes the plan as a minimal unified diff, iteratively refined using a compiler-based Verifier. This division of labor enables robust, policy-aligned adaptations and supports adapting code snippets into a project. On a real-world dataset, AdaptAgent outperforms strong baselines in semantic correctness and produces patches that mirror developers' actual adaptation patterns. Our ablation study shows each agent's necessity, especially planning for code-hardening and exception-handling, and intent for logic customization.

</details>


### [284] [Energy Efficient AI-Enabled Wireless Sensor Networks for Mission Critical Environments: A Systematic Review across Smart Grid, AI, and Urban Infrastructure Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04499)
*Alexandros Gazis,Valeri Mladenov,Kleanthi SantamourI,Stylianos Pappas*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文系统综述了2023至2026年间AI赋能的节能无线传感器网络（WSNs）在任务关键型环境中的研究进展，涵盖电力电子和城市基础设施系统，基于50篇DOI索引研究进行主题编码和比较分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 任务关键型环境对无线传感器网络提出了连续监控、自主运行、可靠通信和快速决策支持的需求，而能源效率不能孤立优化，需要与安全性、延迟和可靠性综合考量。

Method: 采用系统综述方法，收集50篇符合纳入标准的DOI索引研究，进行定性主题编码和比较分析；其他参考文献仅作为历史、方法论或技术背景引用，不纳入综述语料库。

Result: AI可通过路由与聚类、边缘AI、强化学习、模糊逻辑、元启发式优化和AI安全机制改善WSN的能源行为；但能源效率在任务关键型系统中与安全性、延迟和可靠性密切关联，不能作为孤立目标。

Conclusion: 未来研究应脱离孤立优化协议的方向，转而构建轻量级、可解释、安全且经过现场测试的AI驱动WSN架构，以适应真实运行环境。

Abstract: Advanced wireless sensor networks powered by artificial intelligence are increasingly required for applications demanding continuous monitoring, autonomous operation, reliable communication, and fast decision support. This systematic review examines recent work from 2023 to 2026 on energy-efficient, AI-enabled wireless sensor networks (WSNs) in mission-critical environments, with particular focus on power electronics, and urban infrastructure systems. The authors synthesise a corpus of 50 DOI indexed studies satisfying inclusion criteria that received qualitative thematic coding and comparative analysis. Other references were only cited to provide historical, methodological, or technical context and were not included in the systematic review corpus. As such, our results show that AI can improve WSN energy behaviour through routing and clustering, edge AI, reinforcement learning, fuzzy logic, metaheuristic optimisation, and AI-based security. At the same time, energy efficiency cannot be treated as an isolated performance target. In mission-critical systems, security, latency, and reliability are closely interlinked requirements. The review concludes that future work should move away from optimising protocols in isolation, and instead focus on building lightweight, explainable, secure, and field-tested AI-driven WSN architectures suited to real operational environments.

</details>


### [285] [The Order Is the Guarantee: Verifier-Budgeted Code Deletion with Static-First Learned Proposals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04611)
*Ruitong Li,Binjie Guo,Aisheng Mo,Guowei Su,Han Wang,Jie Li,Ru Zhang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文研究AI系统在有限执行验证能力下的代码删除问题，提出DELSCOUT框架，将冗余代码消除形式化为提议调度问题，通过排序器排序单语句删除候选、执行套件验证、预算限制测试次数。实验表明候选顺序（而非模型置信度）是可控制的表面，调度策略决定搜索效率，而测试套件决定行为保持的定义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿编码模型在编程基准上已匹配或超越人类水平，但基准成功并不意味可维护软件。提示驱动的"vibe coding"是增量式的——新分支、守卫和回退逻辑的积累速度远快于过时逻辑的移除。作者研究逆向问题：当执行验证能力有限时，AI系统应如何删除代码。

Method: 将冗余代码减少形式化为提议调度问题：排序器排列单语句删除候选，执行套件接受第一个通过验证的候选，预算限制可测试的候选数量。DELSCOUT实例化了两种调度：1) 有目标域验证时，五槽预算中三槽分配给确定性最短优先候选、两槽分配给互补的学习候选；2) 无验证时先评估完整静态前缀再追加学习候选，确保覆盖率和字符减少单调不减。

Result: 在九个MBPP复现中（使用0.5B、0.6B和8B排序器），五槽预算调度将验证删除覆盖率相对提高9.5%（+6.7个接受任务），同时消耗比匹配的静态基线更少的验证器调用。确定性验证器下覆盖率单调不减，但验证器调用增加4.8-62.5%。MBPP+消除了域内优势，表明调度主导搜索而测试套件单独主导"保持行为"的含义。

Conclusion: 研究产生了一个可审计的分工：模型扩宽可删除代码的搜索空间，顺序限制错误排序提议的损害，执行对每次提交的删除保留最终权威。调度决定搜索，而测试套件决定行为保持的标准，两者分离使系统更可审计和可控。

Abstract: Frontier coding models now match or exceed strong human reference points on programming benchmarks, yet benchmark success does not imply maintainable software. Prompt-driven "vibe coding" is additive: new branches, guards, and fallbacks accumulate faster than obsolete logic is removed. We study the inverse problem-how an Al system should remove code when execution-verification capacity is finite. We formulate redundant-code reduction as proposal scheduling: a ranker orders single-statement deletion candidates, an execution suite accepts the first candidate that passes, and a budget bounds how many candidates may be tested. Our central observation is that candidate order, not model confidence, is the control surface a deployment can reason about. DELSCOUT instantiates two schedules. Given representative target-domain validation, a five-slot budget spends three slots on deterministic shortest-first candidates and two on complementary learned candidates; across nine MBPP replications with 0.5B, 0.6B, and 8B rankers this raises verified-deletion coverage by 9.5% relative (+6.7 accepted tasks) while consuming slightly fewer verifier calls than the matched static baseline. Without such validation the same rankers can lose coverage under shift, so we instead evaluate the complete static prefix first and append learned candidates only afterwards; for a deterministic verifier this makes coverage and character reduction non-decreasing by construction, at a measured 4.8-62.5% increase in verifier calls. MBPP+ then erases the in-domain advantage, showing that scheduling governs search while the test suite alone governs what "preserving behavior" means. The result is an auditable division of labor: models widen the search for removable code, order bounds the damage a mis-ranked proposal can do, and execution retains authority over every committed deletion.

</details>


### [286] [An Exploratory Study of Agent Plans for Agentic AI Coding Tools in Open-Source Software](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04661)
*Muhammad Auwal Abubakar,Seyedmoein Mohsenimofidi,Jai Lal Lulla,Jie M. Zhang,Christoph Treude,Sebastian Baltes,Matthias Galster*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文对开源软件仓库中的Agent Plans进行了探索性研究。研究者筛选了36,710个GitHub仓库，从中识别出10个仓库中的85个Markdown格式的Agent Plan文件。研究发现这些计划文件支持多种软件工程活动（维护、设计、构建、质量相关工作及流程支持），并提供任务导向的执行指导，最常见的是实现步骤、具体文件和位置以及测试验证信息。研究表明，仓库中保存的Agent Plans是研究人机代理工作流中任务意图和执行指导的稀缺但有价值的工件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然已有研究关注AGENTS.md等仓库级共享上下文文件（记录项目级指令和约定），但对Agent Plans这类更面向任务的工件知之甚少。研究者希望填补这一空白，探索Agent Plans在开源仓库中的实际使用情况。

Method: 研究采用探索性方法，筛选了36,710个属于工程化软件项目的GitHub仓库，识别出10个仓库中的85个Markdown格式的Agent Plan文件，并分析了它们的保存方式、支持的开发活动类型以及提供的代理执行指导信息。

Result: Agent Plans在开源仓库中的分布高度集中（85个文件仅来自10个仓库）。这些计划支持多种软件工程活动，包括维护、设计、构建、质量相关工作和流程支持；同时提供任务导向的执行指导，最常见的包括实现步骤、具体文件和位置、以及测试和验证信息。

Conclusion: 仓库中保存的Agent Plans（位于工具特定目录下）是一种范围狭窄但信息丰富的工件，可用于研究人机代理工作流中的任务意图和执行指导。

Abstract: Repository-level configuration artifacts allow developers to provide guidance for agentic AI coding tools, such as Claude Code, Gemini, etc. Although prior research has examined repository-shared context files that capture project-level instructions and conventions (e.g., AGENTS.md files), little is known about more task-oriented artifacts such as Agent Plans. We present an exploratory study of Agent Plans in open-source software repositories, examining how plan files are preserved, which development activities they support, and what information they provide to guide agent execution. We screened 36,710 GitHub repositories belonging to engineered software projects and identified 85 Markdown plan files from 10 repositories. Within this highly concentrated corpus, Agent Plans supported several kinds of software engineering work, including maintenance, design, construction, quality-related work, and process support. They also provided task-oriented execution guidance, most commonly through implementation steps, concrete files and locations, and testing and validation information. Overall, repository-preserved Agent Plans under these tool-specific directories appear to be a narrow but informative artifact for studying task intent and execution guidance in human-agent workflows.

</details>


### [287] [Active-SWE: Benchmarking Coding Agents for Proactive Bug Fixing without Issue Reports](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04682)
*Haobin Li,Ping Deng,Weizhong Qian,Liang Jiang,Zhenyu Huang,Mouxing Yang,Xi Peng*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了Active-SWE基准，用于评估编码智能体在无报告引导下主动发现并修复多个bug的能力，覆盖1,663个任务、六类bug和八种编程语言。实验表明大多数先进的编码智能体在主动bug修复任务中表现有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SWE基准通常假设高质量、信息详细的issue报告总是可用的，但在实际中这种假设很容易被违反，因为报告获取和整理过程复杂。因此需要转向更贴近现实场景的主动bug发现与修复评估。

Method: 提出Active-SWE基准，包含1,663个任务，覆盖六类bug和八种语言；设计了一种新颖的难度感知任务构建流水线，并采用双轨评估框架，从修复已记录bug和发现潜在bug两个维度进行综合评估。

Result: 大量实验显示，大多数最先进的编码智能体在主动bug修复任务中表现困难，在定位和修复已记录bug、处理多bug修复场景以及发现有效潜在bug方面能力有限。

Conclusion: 编码智能体从被动bug修复转向主动bug发现和修复仍有很大提升空间，Active-SWE为该方向提供了更全面的评估基准。

Abstract: Coding agents powered by large language models (LLMs) are increasingly adopted in software engineering (SWE) scenarios, capable of fixing a specific bug in large-scale codebase. However, existing SWE benchmarks typically assume that high-quality issue reports with detailed information are always available, which is easily violated in practice due to the complexity of report acquisition and curation. To address this, we introduce Active-SWE, a benchmark for evaluating coding agents on proactively discovering and fixing multiple bugs without report guidance, covering 1,663 tasks across six bug categories and eight languages. Beyond shifting the focus from existing reactive bug fixing to proactive bug fixing, Active-SWE enables a more in-depth evaluation by expanding the scope from fixing a specific recorded bug to multiple-bug fixing and potential bug discovery scenarios. To construct Active-SWE, we propose a novel difficulty-aware task formulation pipeline with a dual-track evaluation framework, facilitating comprehensive evaluation of proactive bug-fixing capability. Extensive experiments reveal that most state-of-the-art coding agents struggle with proactive bug-fixing tasks, demonstrating limited performance in locating and resolving recorded bugs, handling multiple bug fixing scenarios, and discovering valid potential bugs.

</details>


### [288] [What We Observe as LLM Behavior Can Be a Side-effect of Inference Backend](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04714)
*Shahed Masoudian,Passant Shafaei,Monorama Swain,Markus Schedl*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文研究了推理框架（如HuggingFace、vLLM、Ollama等）对模型基准测试分数的影响，发现后端选择是一个不可忽视的因素，即使在贪心解码下也能显著改变模型性能，建议在报告基准分数时披露后端、版本及完整生成配置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基准测试分数通常被视为模型本身的属性，但推理框架（后端）的选择被认为无影响且几乎从不披露，作者希望探究这一选择对模型输出的实际影响程度。

Method: 采用完全交叉实验设计：三个指令微调模型 × 五个推理框架 × 六个基准测试 × 四种生成模式，系统地隔离后端（包装器/底层引擎）、采样噪声和默认生成参数对分数的影响，并进行方差分解分析。

Result: 后端是不可忽视的因素，即使在无采样噪声的贪心解码下，更换后端也能显著改变模型性能，且该效应是结构性的且强烈依赖模型。方差分解显示约39%的变异性源于后端，其余来自采样噪声和各框架默认生成参数。事实性基准上的分歧比社会偏见基准更明显。

Conclusion: 基准测试分数并非与后端无关，建议在报告分数时披露后端、版本及完整生成配置，并使用确定性解码进行跨后端比较。

Abstract: Benchmark scores are reported as properties of a model, yet the inference framework used to produce them, such as HuggingFace, vLLM, or Ollama, are considered non-influential and their names and versions are almost never disclosed. In this work we investigate how much this choice can influence the model output. In a fully-crossed study (three instruction-tuned models x five inference frameworks x six benchmarks x four generation modes) we investigate how different tools (wrappers/backend) influence benchmark scores and how their score changes is influenced by generation hyper-parameters. We find backend to be a non-negligible factor where even under greedy, sampling-noise-free decoding, changing the backend can significantly alter models performance and this effect is structural and strongly model-dependent. Decomposing the variance according to generation mode reveal that considerable portion of the variability (roughly 39\%) a practitioner sees out-of-the-box can stem from the backend, while the remaining stems from sampling noise and each framework's default generation parameters, both of which are avoidable by disclosing and matching the generation configuration. These divergences are more pronounced on factual than on social-bias benchmarks. Overall, benchmark numbers are not backend-agnostic therefore, we recommend disclosing the backend, its version, and the full generation configuration, also using deterministic decoding for cross-backend comparison.

</details>


### [289] [RepoProbe: Benchmarking Architecture-Aware Repository Comprehension with Checklists](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04783)
*Yuexi Yang,Alyssa Wu,Ji Luo,Richeng Xuan,Zhichao Hu,Yuhong Liu,Zhen Qin*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: RepoProbe是一个用于评估LLM仓库级代码理解能力的新型基准，基于GitHub Discussions中的开放式架构问题，并采用基于清单的验证协议替代传统标量评分，揭示SOTA LLM在清晰度与事实准确性之间存在差距，并量化确认了"编辑偏见"（模型过早生成代码而非理解架构）的普遍性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准依赖GitHub Issues中的bug报告，允许模型通过错误日志模式匹配绕过真实理解，导致对"编辑偏见"（过早生成代码而非理解仓库架构）的低估；同时LLM-as-a-Judge的标量评分存在高方差和低可解释性问题。

Method: 提出RepoProbe基准：1) 使用GitHub Discussions中的开放式架构问题而非缺陷报告；2) 提出基于清单的验证协议，将答案分解为原子可验证事实，用客观验证替代主观评分。

Result: 对SOTA LLM的评估显示：模型在清晰度与基于证据的技术正确性之间存在持续差距；定量确认了编辑偏见的普遍性（模型优先代码生成而非架构分析）；验证协议相比传统标量评分的评估可靠性有显著提升。

Conclusion: RepoProbe通过开放式架构问答和清单式验证协议，提供了更准确、更可靠的仓库级代码理解评估方法，揭示并量化了编辑偏见问题，为未来LLM在软件工程中的应用评估提供了新范式。

Abstract: The integration of Large Language Models (LLMs) into software engineering has shifted the focus from function-level generation to repository-scale assistance. However, existing benchmarks largely rely on bug reports from GitHub Issues, which often allow models to bypass genuine understanding via pattern matching on error logs. This misalignment under-measures Edit Bias, which refers to premature generation, where models prematurely propose code modifications instead of understanding the existing repository architecture. Furthermore, current LLM-as-a-Judge scalar scoring suffers from high variance and low interpretability. This work introduces RepoProbe, a novel benchmark for evaluating repository-level code understanding through open-ended Q&A using GitHub Discussions, which focuses on open-ended architectural inquiries rather than defect reporting. To ensure rigorous evaluation, we propose a Checklist-Based Verification Protocol that decomposes answers into atomic, verifiable facts, thereby replacing subjective ratings with objective verification. Our evaluation of state-of-the-art (SOTA) LLMs reveals a persistent gap between high clarity and evidencegrounded technical correctness. It also quantitatively confirms the prevalence of edit bias, in which models prioritize code generation instead of architectural analysis. Finally, we demonstrate that our verification protocol significantly improves evaluation reliability compared to traditional evaluations with scalar scoring.

</details>


### [290] [Scrouting: Cost-Aware Routing of Coding Agents by Scouting the Repository First](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04804)
*Ishaan Bhola,Adithyan Krishnan,Mukunda NS*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: SuperScout是一种通过先侦察仓库再进行模型路由的框架：一个7B搜索模型探索仓库并生成经过沙盒验证的结构化交接信息，结合其隐藏状态和任务文本，由基于简历的路由器将任务分配给四个前沿修复模型之一。在SWE-bench Pro基准上，SuperScout以约五分之一的成本达到与最佳单模型相当的解决率，但消融实验表明交接信息本身而非路由决策承担了主要效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿语言模型可以解决仓库级软件问题，但每次尝试成本高昂，而现有路由器仅从问题文本本身选择模型，缺乏对仓库的深入理解，导致路由效率低下和成本浪费。

Method: SuperScout由两个核心组件构成：(1) SuperScout-7B搜索器，先探索仓库并生成结构化交接信息，其中复现声称经过沙盒验证，虚假声称在传递前被剔除；(2) 基于简历的路由器，利用搜索器的隐藏状态和任务文本，将任务分配到四个前沿修复模型之一。添加新的修复模型无需重新训练。

Result: 在SWE-bench Pro的完整Python切片（266个任务）上，SuperScout解决159个任务，超过最佳单模型的158个，而每个解决的成本约为其五分之一。无路由消融实验（始终使用最便宜修复模型+交接信息）与路由系统持平，表明交接信息而非路由决策带来了主要收益。配对校准研究显示交接信息重新分配而非增加解决能力，提升了三个较便宜修复器的性能而略微损害最强修复器，但N=99时各修复器效应仅为方向性。搜索器的GPU计算成本每次任务不到半美分。

Conclusion: SuperScout证明了"先侦察后路由"范式的潜力，以显著更低的成本实现与最佳单模型相当的解决率。然而，路由决策本身并未带来额外收益，交接信息的生成才是核心贡献；搜索器的隐藏状态对校准标签上的成本路由有改善作用，但交接文本本身无此效果。

Abstract: Frontier language models can resolve repository-level software issues, but each attempt is expensive, and existing routers select a model from the issue text alone. We present SuperScout, which routes after scouting the repository: a 7B searcher, SuperScout-7B, first explores the repository and produces a structured handoff whose reproduction claims are sandbox-verified, with false claims stripped before delivery. The searcher's hidden states, together with the task text, then feed a resume-based router that dispatches the task to one of four frontier fixers. Adding a new fixer requires no retraining. On the full Python slice of SWE-bench Pro (266 tasks) under the benchmark's official capped budget tier, SuperScout matches the best single model's solve rate (159 of 266 for SuperScout, 158 for the best model) at about a fifth of the total cost per solve, and the reported configuration sits above the random traffic-splitting baseline. A no-router ablation, always the cheapest fixer with the handoff, ties the routed system on this benchmark, so the handoff rather than the routing decision carries the result. A paired calibration study points to the mechanism: the handoff appears to redistribute rather than add solving ability, lifting the three cheaper fixers while slightly hurting the strongest, though at $N=99$ the per-fixer effects are directional only; the searcher's hidden states improve cost routing on the calibration labels while the handoff's own text does not. The searcher's compute adds less than half a cent of GPU time per task.

</details>


### [291] [A Chain Is Only as Strong as Its Weakest Link: A Scoping Review of System Integration Audits in AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04921)
*Leah Davis,Dominic Martin,AJung Moon*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过范围综述（扫描4,259篇文献，筛选出58篇），探讨了以系统集成为核心原则的AI审计实践，发现这是一个新兴但碎片化的领域，集成审计可分为组件间、系统-环境和多系统三个层次，涵盖风险探索、风险判定、协调和程序规范性等功能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI系统日益融入各类接口和应用，仅关注模型本身的审计已不足以应对系统组件与部署环境交互所产生的风险。系统集成虽在航空航天等安全关键领域是软件审计的核心，但在AI审计中尚未得到充分探索。

Method: 作者扫描了4,259份文档，筛选出58篇将系统集成作为评估核心原则的AI审计研究，并采用反思性主题分析（reflexive thematic analysis）对其要素、参与者、使能因素和约束条件进行系统分析。

Result: 研究发现：现有AI集成审计数量有限且碎片化；很少有针对集成特定风险的措施；与传统审计期望之间存在较大差距；信息和资源的可获取性显著影响审计设计。集成审计可归类为三个层次（组件间、系统-环境、多系统），分别承担风险探索、风险判定、协调和程序规范性功能，并评估兼容性、完整性和监督等集成特定属性。

Conclusion: 作者呼吁AI社区将系统集成作为应对AI风险的核心策略，并发展能够捕捉组件、环境和系统层面失败的审计实践，以超越仅凭组件级评估所能覆盖的范围。

Abstract: As AI systems become increasingly integrated into diverse interfaces and applications, model-centric audits are insufficient to address risks arising from interactions among system components and deployment environments. System integration has long been central to software audits in safety-critical domains such as aerospace. However, its role in AI auditing remains underexplored. Scanning through 4,259 documents, we present a scoping review of AI audits that treat system integration as a core tenet of evaluation (n = 58). Using reflexive thematic analysis, we analyze their elements, actors, enablers, and constraints. We find that the corpus represents an emerging yet still fragmented form of AI auditing: few existing measures target integration-specific risks; large gaps remain in meeting traditional audit expectations; and access to necessary information and resources significantly influences audit design. Nonetheless, integration can be categorized across three sites (inter-component, system-environment, and multi-system), each serving the functions of risk exploration, risk determination, coordination, and procedural regularity. Deviating from other types of evaluations, these audits assess qualities specific to system integration, including compatibility, completeness, and oversight. This review calls on the AI community to prioritize system integration as a core strategy for addressing AI risk, and to develop audit practices capable of capturing failures across components, environments, and systems beyond the reach of component-level evaluation.

</details>


### [292] [SciCode-Verified: How Benchmark Defects Underestimated the Scientific-Coding Ability of Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04975)
*Sihan Hu,Lyuhan Huang,Youjin Deng,Kun Chen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文发现SciCode评测基准存在严重缺陷（65个测试问题中发现263个缺陷，其中192个导致正确解答被错误拒绝），通过领域专家逐题审计并修正后构建了SciCode-Verified基准。在修正后的基准上，12个前沿模型的子问题准确率从45-60%提升至84-98%，主问题准确率从9-27%提升至69-92%，表明此前性能瓶颈源于评测工具质量问题而非模型能力不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SciCode是衡量语言模型科学编码能力的标准基准，被广泛用于政府和国家实验室评估，但近期模型得分出现平台期（最强模型聚集在60%左右），作者怀疑问题出在基准本身而非模型能力。

Method: 对全部65个测试问题逐题进行领域专家审计，发现263个缺陷（其中192个导致正确解答被错误拒绝，78%需专业物理/数学知识才能发现）；随后修正所有可确认的缺陷，构建SciCode-Verified，每次修改都记录理由并由第二位领域专家独立复核，最后重新评估12个前沿模型。

Result: 修正后子问题准确率从45-60%上升至84-98%，主问题准确率从9-27%上升至69-92%。导致分数被压制的缺陷中91%分布在主问题上，缺陷类型包括金标准答案不可复现、容忍度过严、规格自相矛盾等。

Conclusion: 模型在科学编码上的表现远优于SciCode所显示的水平，瓶颈不在于模型能力而在于评测工具质量。作者发布SciCode-Verified及其完整审计记录作为修正后的公共标准。

Abstract: SciCode is the standard measure of the scientific-coding ability of language models: research-level problems that demand both frontier scientific theory and its implementation as working numerical code. It is a component of the Artificial Analysis Intelligence Index and a standing evaluation in government and national-laboratory suites. Yet its scores have recently plateaued: the strongest 2026 models cluster tightly around 60\% subproblem accuracy, and a successor model ties its predecessor. We trace this stagnation to defects in the benchmark itself. A per-problem, domain-expert audit of all 65 test problems uncovers 263 defects; 192 of them, spread across 91\% of the main problems, cause correct, instruction-following solutions to be wrongly rejected---through non-reproducible gold answers, over-tight tolerances, or self-contradictory specifications. Critically, 78\% of these score-suppressing defects require specialized physics or mathematics knowledge to detect, not mere clerical proofreading. We corrected every confirmable defect to produce SciCode-Verified. The corrections add only the specifications a well-posed problem requires, repair grading, and tighten the tests that were too lenient; every change is recorded with its justification and independently re-checked by a second domain expert. We re-evaluate twelve frontier model snapshots on the corrected benchmark and find a substantial recovery: subproblem accuracy rises from 45--60\% to 84--98\%, and main-problem accuracy from 9--27\% to 69--92\%. State-of-the-art models are far more proficient in scientific coding than SciCode has suggested---the bottleneck was not model capability, but the quality of the evaluation instrument. We release SciCode-Verified with its complete audit trail as the corrected public standard.

</details>


### [293] [RepairFormer: Automated Repair of Structured Inputs Using Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05060)
*Ovi Paul,Tom J King,Ali Shokri*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出RepairFormer，一种基于Transformer的框架，用于修复结构化输入文件（如JSON、DOT、OBJ等）中的损坏。该框架将修复建模为监督式序列生成任务，结合格式标签、oracle验证和边界局部化修复，在保持内容的前提下生成有效输出。实验显示修复率88%、恢复率94%，在基准测试中达到97.57%修复率和94.29%恢复率，运行速度比现有最佳方案快5倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结构化输入文件（JSON、DOT、OBJ等）在软件系统中广泛使用，但微小的损坏会导致解析器拒绝整体数据，从而中断测试、分析、部署等自动化流程。现有修复技术虽能产生结构有效的输入，但常依赖删除或重复搜索，会丢失原始内容并导致语义不正确。

Method: RepairFormer采用Transformer架构，将输入修复形式化为监督式序列生成任务。核心工作流包括：使用格式标签辅助生成、利用oracle验证修复结果、以及采用边界局部化修复（boundary-localized repair）——聚焦检测到的故障区域进行生成，缩小输入规模并支持更长文件的修复。

Result: 在评估中，RepairFormer在修复方面达到88%，恢复方面达到94%，且在修复成功时显示出最强的内容保留能力。在额外基准测试中，RepairFormer实现了97.57%的修复率和94.29%的恢复率，运行时间比现有技术快5倍。

Conclusion: RepairFormer通过将修复视为序列生成任务并引入边界局部化策略，有效平衡了结构有效性与内容保留之间的矛盾。相比现有方法，它在修复率、恢复率和运行效率上均取得显著提升，为结构化输入修复提供了更可靠的解决方案。

Abstract: Structured input files such as JSON, DOT, OBJ, INI, S-expression, and TinyC are widely used in software systems, but small corruptions can cause parsers to reject otherwise useful data. Repairing such inputs is important because malformed configuration, program, and data files can interrupt testing, analysis, deployment, and downstream automation even when most of the original content remains intact. Existing repair techniques can produce structurally valid inputs, but they often rely on deletion or repeated search, which may lose original content and result in semantic incorrectness. This paper presents RepairFormer, a transformer-based framework for structured input repair. The approach formulates repair as a supervised sequence generation task and uses format tags, oracle validation, and boundary-localized repair to generate valid outputs while preserving content. The boundary workflow focuses generation on the detected fault region, reducing the input size, and supporting repair of longer files. In evaluation, RepairFormer achieves a 88% in repair and 94% in recovery, showing strongest content preservation when repairs are successful. Additional experiments on our benchmark shows RepairFormer repairs 97.57% and recovers 94.29% with 5x faster runtime compared to state of the art.

</details>


### [294] [Characterizing Visual Accessibility Issues in AI Developer Tools: An Empirical Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05116)
*Sabrina Haque,Christoph Csallner*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该研究分析了五个AI开发者工具生态系统（GitHub Copilot、Cursor、Claude Code、OpenAI Codex和OpenCode）中的可访问性问题报告，通过2,652个检索候选和600份确认的可访问性报告，识别出三类主要障碍：屏幕阅读器与辅助技术问题、视觉呈现与对比度问题、以及AI特定界面的可读性和控制问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI辅助开发者工具越来越多地通过聊天面板、终端代理、生成的差异和流式状态输出进行编程交互，但这些交互界面可能对盲人、低视力和色觉缺陷开发者造成视觉可访问性障碍，而目前对此类障碍在公共工具生态系统中的报告情况知之甚少。

Method: 从五个AI开发者工具生态系统（GitHub Copilot、Cursor、Claude Code、OpenAI Codex和OpenCode）检索2,652个候选问题及论坛讨论，使用三模型集成从中识别出600个一致确认的视觉可访问性报告，并通过分层人工抽查验证保守选择的有效性，随后采用主题建模和定性分析进行分类。

Result: 识别出三类反复出现的可访问性问题：屏幕阅读器和辅助技术障碍；视觉呈现、对比度和区分度问题；AI特定界面中的可读性、缩放和控制限制。这些问题在不同生态系统中突出程度不同，反映了编辑器、终端、聊天、差异和代理交互界面的差异。探索性元数据分析还发现了报告者活跃度以及维护者参与和关闭流程的差异。

Conclusion: AI开发者工具的可访问性记录受其交互设计和周边生态系统的报告与维护实践共同影响，表明改善这些工具的无障碍性不仅需要关注界面设计本身，还需要关注生态系统的报告和维护机制。

Abstract: AI-assisted developer tools increasingly mediate programming through chat panels, terminal agents, generated diffs, and streaming status output. These interaction surfaces may create visual accessibility barriers for blind, low-vision, and color-vision-deficient developers, yet little is known about how such barriers are reported in public tool ecosystems. We analyze issues and forum discussions from five AI developer tool ecosystems: GitHub Copilot in VS Code, Cursor, Claude Code, OpenAI Codex, and OpenCode. From 2,652 keyword-retrieved candidates, a three-model ensemble identified 600 unanimously positive visual accessibility reports. A stratified manual sanity check supported this conservative selection. Topic modeling and qualitative analysis identified three recurring categories: screen-reader and assistive-technology barriers; visual presentation, contrast, and differentiation problems; and readability, scaling, and control limitations in AI-specific interfaces. The relative prominence of these concerns varied across ecosystems and reflected differences in editor, terminal, chat, diff, and agent interaction surfaces. An exploratory metadata analysis further identified differences in reporter activity and, across the GitHub-based ecosystems, maintainer participation and closure processes. These findings show that the accessibility record of AI developer tools is shaped by both their interaction design and the reporting and maintenance practices of their surrounding ecosystems.

</details>
